const BASE_URL = "http://192.168.1.226:3000"; 

export async function request(path, options = {}) {
  const method = options.method ?? "GET";

  const headers = {
    "Content-Type": "application/json",
    ...(options.headers ?? {}),
  };

  if (options.token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${options.token}`;

  const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers,
    body: options.body ? JSON.stringify(options.body) : undefined,
  });

  const text = await res.text();
  const data = text ? safeJson(text) : null;

  if (!res.ok) {
    const msg =
      (data && (data.message || data.error)) ||
      `Request failed (${res.status})`;
    throw new Error(msg);
  }

  return data;
}

function safeJson(text) {
  try { return JSON.parse(text); } catch { return null; }
}

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import { View, Text, ImageBackground, SafeAreaView, StyleSheet, Platform, StatusBar } from "react-native";
import { useFonts, BrunoAceSC_400Regular } from "@expo-google-fonts/bruno-ace-sc";
import { Stack } from "expo-router";

import TopBar from "../../layout/TopBar";
import BottomBar from "../../layout/BottomBar";
import Fab from "../../layout/fab/Fab";

import MainHomePage from "../../features/drive/pages/MainHomePage";
import StarredPage from "../../features/drive/pages/StarredPage";
import SharedPage from "../../features/drive/pages/SharedPage";
import MyDrivePage from "../../features/drive/pages/MyDrivePage";

import { getDriveStyles } from "../../features/drive/styles/myDrive.styles";
import { useTheme } from "../providers/ThemeContext";

import { subscribeNavigateToFolder } from "../../features/drive/services/driveEvents";

// Images
const lightBg = require("../../assets/images/light.png");
const nightBg = require("../../assets/images/night.png");

export default function HomeManager() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("home");
  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({ BrunoAceSC_400Regular });

  const { isDarkMode, colors } = useTheme();
  const styles = useMemo(() => getDriveStyles(colors), [colors]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeNavigateToFolder(() => {
      setActiveTab("mydrive");
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, []);


  const handleRefresh = () => {
    console.log("Global refresh triggered via FAB");
  };

  if (!fontsLoaded) return null;

  const getTitle = () => {
    switch(activeTab) {
      case 'home': return 'HOME';
      case 'starred': return 'STARRED';
      case 'shared': return 'SHARED';
      case 'mydrive': return 'MY DRIVE';
      default: return 'COPA DRIVE';
    }
  };

  return (
    <View style={localStyles.container}>
      <Stack.Screen options={{ headerShown: false }} />
      
      <ImageBackground
        source={isDarkMode ? nightBg : lightBg}
        style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
        resizeMode="cover"
      />

      <SafeAreaView style={localStyles.safeArea}>
        
        {/* TopBar */}
        <View style={{ zIndex: 100 }}> 
          <TopBar />
        </View>

        {/* ג… ׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡׳™׳™׳” ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™׳× - ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳” ׳׳₪׳™ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳׳™׳׳”/׳™׳•׳ */}
        <View style={[
            localStyles.titleCard, 
            { 
              // ׳׳™׳׳”: ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳›׳”׳” | ׳™׳•׳: ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳׳‘׳ ׳—׳׳‘׳™
              backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? 'rgba(20, 20, 30, 0.6)' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65)',
              // ׳׳™׳׳”: ׳׳¡׳’׳¨׳× ׳“׳§׳” ׳׳‘׳ ׳” | ׳™׳•׳: ׳׳¡׳’׳¨׳× ׳׳‘׳ ׳” ׳—׳–׳§׳” ׳™׳•׳×׳¨
              borderColor: isDarkMode ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4)'
            }
        ]}>
          <Text style={[
              styles.homeTitle, 
              localStyles.customTitleText,
              {
                // ׳׳™׳׳”: ׳˜׳§׳¡׳˜ ׳׳‘׳ | ׳™׳•׳: ׳˜׳§׳¡׳˜ ׳›׳”׳” (׳›׳—׳•׳ ׳›׳”׳”/׳©׳—׳•׳¨) ׳›׳“׳™ ׳©׳™׳”׳™׳” ׳§׳¨׳™׳
                color: isDarkMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#1A2A3A',
                // ׳¦׳ ׳׳˜׳§׳¡׳˜ ׳¨׳§ ׳‘׳׳™׳׳”. ׳‘׳™׳•׳ ׳–׳” ׳ ׳¨׳׳” ׳׳׳•׳›׳׳ ׳¢׳ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳׳‘׳
                textShadowColor: isDarkMode ? 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)' : 'transparent',
              }
          ]}>
            {getTitle()}
          </Text>
        </View>

        {/* ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳”׳¢׳׳•׳“ */}
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          {activeTab === "home" && <MainHomePage />}
          {activeTab === "starred" && <StarredPage />}
          {activeTab === "shared" && <SharedPage />}
          {activeTab === "mydrive" && <MyDrivePage />}
        </View>

      </SafeAreaView>

      {/* FAB */}
      <View style={localStyles.fabWrapper} pointerEvents="box-none">
         <Fab onRefresh={handleRefresh} />
      </View>

      {/* BottomBar */}
      <View style={localStyles.bottomBarWrapper}>
        <BottomBar activeTab={activeTab} setActiveTab={setActiveTab} />
      </View>

    </View>
  );
}

  titleCard: {

===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\app\(app)\important.jsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View,
  StyleSheet,
  SafeAreaView,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Text,
  FlatList,
  ActivityIndicator,
  StatusBar,
  Alert,
  Pressable
} from "react-native";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { useAuth } from "../providers/AuthContext";
import { getImportantFiles, moveToTrash } from "../../features/drive/services/fileService";
import DriveListItem from "../../features/drive/components/DriveListItem";

// ג… ׳™׳™׳‘׳•׳ Theme
import { getDriveStyles } from "../../features/drive/styles/myDrive.styles";
import { useTheme } from "../providers/ThemeContext";

import { subscribeDriveRefresh, emitDriveRefresh } from "../../features/drive/services/driveEvents";
import FilePreviewModal from "../../features/drive/components/FilePreviewModal";

export default function ImportantPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { token } = useAuth();
  
  // ג… Theme
  const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme();
  const driveStyles = useMemo(() => getDriveStyles(colors), [colors]);

  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  
  // ג… ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳™ ׳ ׳™׳•׳•׳˜ ׳×׳™׳§׳™׳•׳×
  const [currentFolderId, setCurrentFolderId] = useState(null);
  const [folderPath, setFolderPath] = useState([]);

  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null);

  const loadImportant = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!token) return;
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const data = await getImportantFiles(token);
      setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to load important files:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [token]);

  useEffect(() => { loadImportant(); }, [loadImportant]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeDriveRefresh(() => loadImportant());
    return unsubscribe;
  }, [loadImportant]);

  // ג… ׳¡׳™׳ ׳•׳ ׳”׳×׳¦׳•׳’׳”: ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳—׳ ׳• ׳‘׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳”, ׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳©׳׳”. ׳׳—׳¨׳×, ׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳× ׳”׳—׳©׳•׳‘׳™׳.
  const displayItems = useMemo(() => {
    if (currentFolderId) {
       // ׳‘׳×׳•׳ ׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳” - ׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳׳” ׳©׳‘׳×׳•׳›׳” (׳׳×׳•׳ ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳× ׳”׳—׳©׳•׳‘׳™׳)
       // ׳”׳¢׳¨׳”: ׳׳ ׳¨׳•׳¦׳™׳ ׳׳¨׳׳•׳× ׳׳× *׳›׳* ׳”׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳” (׳’׳ ׳׳ ׳—׳©׳•׳‘׳™׳), ׳–׳” ׳“׳•׳¨׳© ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× API ׳׳—׳¨׳×.
       // ׳›׳׳ ׳׳ ׳™ ׳׳ ׳™׳— ׳©׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳₪׳¨׳™׳˜׳™׳ ׳—׳©׳•׳‘׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳׳¦׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳” ׳”׳–׳•.
       return items.filter(item => item.parentId === currentFolderId);
    }
    // ׳‘׳¨׳©׳™׳׳” ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳™׳× - ׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳”׳›׳
    return items;
  }, [items, currentFolderId]);

  const closeScreen = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      if (typeof router.canGoBack === "function" && router.canGoBack()) {
        router.back();
      } else { router.replace("/home"); }
    } catch { router.replace("/home"); }
  }, [router]);

  const openItem = useCallback((item) => {
    if (!item) return;
    if (item.type === "folder") {
      // ג… ׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ׳׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳”
      setCurrentFolderId(item.id);
      setFolderPath((prev) => [...prev, { id: item.id, name: item.name }]);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedId(item.id);
    setPreviewOpen(true);
  }, []);

  const handleDeleteItem = useCallback(async (item) => {
    Alert.alert("Move to Trash?", `Remove "${item.name}"?`, [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      {
        text: "Delete", style: "destructive",
        onPress: async () => {
          try { await moveToTrash(token, item.id); emitDriveRefresh(); } 
          catch (err) { Alert.alert("Error", "Could not delete item."); }
        }
      }
    ]);
  }, [token]);

  // ג… ׳—׳–׳¨׳” ׳׳—׳•׳¨׳” ׳‘׳×׳™׳§׳™׳•׳×
  const handleBack = useCallback(() => {
    if (folderPath.length === 0) return;
    const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, -1);
    setFolderPath(newPath);
    setCurrentFolderId(newPath.length > 0 ? newPath[newPath.length - 1].id : null);
  }, [folderPath]);

  // ג… ׳ ׳™׳•׳•׳˜ ׳‘׳₪׳™׳¨׳•׳¨׳™׳
  const navigateToBreadcrumb = useCallback((index) => {
    if (index < 0) {
      setFolderPath([]);
      setCurrentFolderId(null);
    } else {
      const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, index + 1);
      setFolderPath(newPath);
      setCurrentFolderId(newPath[newPath.length - 1].id);
    }
  }, [folderPath]);

  return (
    <View style={[styles.modalContainer, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={isDarkMode ? "light-content" : "dark-content"} />
      <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
          <View style={styles.headerLeftSpace} />
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={closeScreen}
            style={[styles.closeButton, { backgroundColor: colors.surface }]}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <Ionicons name="close-outline" size={32} color={colors.textSecondary} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.content}>
          <View style={styles.textHeader}>
            <Text style={[driveStyles.homeTitle, { marginLeft: 0 }]}>IMPORTANT</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.subTitle, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Items marked with high priority</Text>
          </View>

          {/* ג… Breadcrumb Navigation */}
          {folderPath.length > 0 && (
            <View style={driveStyles.breadcrumbContainer}>
            <Pressable onPress={handleBack} style={driveStyles.backButton}>
                <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable onPress={() => navigateToBreadcrumb(-1)}>
                <Text style={driveStyles.breadcrumbText}>Important</Text>
            </Pressable>

            {folderPath.map((folder, index) => (
                <View key={folder.id} style={driveStyles.breadcrumbItem}>
                <Text style={driveStyles.breadcrumbSeparator}>/</Text>
                <Pressable onPress={() => navigateToBreadcrumb(index)}>
                    <Text style={[
                    driveStyles.breadcrumbText,
                    index === folderPath.length - 1 && driveStyles.breadcrumbActive
                    ]} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {folder.name}
                    </Text>
                </Pressable>
                </View>
            ))}
            </View>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: 50 }} />
          ) : (
            <FlatList
              data={displayItems}
              keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
              renderItem={({ item }) => (
                <DriveListItem 
                    item={item} 
                    onPress={openItem} 
                    onDelete={handleDeleteItem}
                />
              )}
              contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}
              ListEmptyComponent={
                <View style={styles.emptyContainer}>
                  <Ionicons name="alert-circle-outline" size={80} color={colors.textTertiary} />
                  <Text style={[driveStyles.emptyText, { color: colors.textTertiary }]}>
                      {currentFolderId ? "No important items in this folder" : "No important items yet"}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              }
              onRefresh={loadImportant}
              refreshing={loading}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            />
          )}
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>

      <FilePreviewModal
        open={previewOpen}
        fileId={selectedId}
        onClose={() => setPreviewOpen(false)}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  modalContainer: { flex: 1 },
  modalHeader: {
    flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 15, height: 60,
  },
  headerLeftSpace: { width: 40 },
  closeButton: {
    width: 44, height: 44, justifyContent: "center", alignItems: "center", borderRadius: 22,
  },
  content: { flex: 1 },
  textHeader: { paddingHorizontal: 20, marginBottom: 15 },
  subTitle: { fontSize: 13, marginTop: -8, marginLeft: 0 },
  emptyContainer: { alignItems: "center", marginTop: 100 },
});

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View,
  StyleSheet,
  SafeAreaView,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Text,
  FlatList,
  ActivityIndicator,
  StatusBar,
  Alert,
  Pressable
} from "react-native";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { useAuth } from "../providers/AuthContext";
import {
  getRecentFiles,
  formatLastAccessed,
  moveToTrash
} from "../../features/drive/services/fileService";
import DriveListItem from "../../features/drive/components/DriveListItem";

// ג… Theme Imports
import { getDriveStyles } from "../../features/drive/styles/myDrive.styles";
import { useTheme } from "../providers/ThemeContext";

import { subscribeDriveRefresh, emitDriveRefresh } from "../../features/drive/services/driveEvents";
import FilePreviewModal from "../../features/drive/components/FilePreviewModal";

export default function RecentPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { token } = useAuth();
  
  // ג… Theme Hooks
  const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme();
  const driveStyles = useMemo(() => getDriveStyles(colors), [colors]);

  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  
  // ג… ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳™ ׳ ׳™׳•׳•׳˜
  const [currentFolderId, setCurrentFolderId] = useState(null);
  const [folderPath, setFolderPath] = useState([]);

  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null);

  const loadRecent = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!token) return;
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const data = await getRecentFiles(token);
      setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to load recent files:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [token]);

  useEffect(() => { loadRecent(); }, [loadRecent]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeDriveRefresh(() => loadRecent());
    return unsubscribe;
  }, [loadRecent]);

  // ג… ׳¡׳™׳ ׳•׳ ׳₪׳¨׳™׳˜׳™׳ (׳׳ ׳‘׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳” - ׳׳¦׳™׳’ ׳׳× ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳”, ׳׳—׳¨׳× ׳׳¦׳™׳’ ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳”׳׳—׳¨׳•׳ ׳™׳)
  const displayItems = useMemo(() => {
    if (currentFolderId) {
        return items.filter(item => item.parentId === currentFolderId);
    }
    return items;
  }, [items, currentFolderId]);

  const closeScreen = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      if (typeof router.canGoBack === "function" && router.canGoBack()) {
        router.back();
      } else { router.replace("/home"); }
    } catch { router.replace("/home"); }
  }, [router]);

      setCurrentFolderId(item.id);
      setFolderPath((prev) => [...prev, { id: item.id, name: item.name }]);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedId(item.id);
    setPreviewOpen(true);
  }, []);

  const handleDeleteItem = useCallback(async (item) => {
    Alert.alert("Move to Trash?", `Remove "${item.name}"?`, [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      {
        text: "Delete", style: "destructive",
        onPress: async () => {
          try { await moveToTrash(token, item.id); emitDriveRefresh(); } 
          catch (err) { Alert.alert("Error", "Could not delete item."); }
        }
      }
    ]);
  }, [token]);

  // ג… ׳—׳–׳¨׳” ׳׳—׳•׳¨׳” ׳‘׳×׳™׳§׳™׳•׳×
  const handleBack = useCallback(() => {
    if (folderPath.length === 0) return;
    const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, -1);
    setFolderPath(newPath);
    setCurrentFolderId(newPath.length > 0 ? newPath[newPath.length - 1].id : null);
  }, [folderPath]);

  // ג… ׳ ׳™׳•׳•׳˜ ׳‘׳₪׳™׳¨׳•׳¨׳™׳
  const navigateToBreadcrumb = useCallback((index) => {
    if (index < 0) {
      setFolderPath([]);
      setCurrentFolderId(null);
    } else {
      const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, index + 1);
      setFolderPath(newPath);
      setCurrentFolderId(newPath[newPath.length - 1].id);
    }
  }, [folderPath]);

  return (
    <View style={[styles.modalContainer, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={isDarkMode ? "light-content" : "dark-content"} />
      <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
          <View style={styles.headerLeftSpace} />

            onPress={closeScreen} 
            style={[styles.closeButton, { backgroundColor: colors.surface }]}
          >
            <Ionicons name="close-outline" size={32} color={colors.textSecondary} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.content}>
          <View style={styles.textHeader}>
            <Text style={[driveStyles.homeTitle, { marginLeft: 0, paddingLeft: 0 }]}>
              RECENT
            </Text>

          </View>

            <View style={driveStyles.breadcrumbContainer}>
            <Pressable onPress={handleBack} style={driveStyles.backButton}>
                <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable onPress={() => navigateToBreadcrumb(-1)}>
                <Text style={driveStyles.breadcrumbText}>Recent</Text>
            </Pressable>

            {folderPath.map((folder, index) => (
                <View key={folder.id} style={driveStyles.breadcrumbItem}>
                <Text style={driveStyles.breadcrumbSeparator}>/</Text>
                <Pressable onPress={() => navigateToBreadcrumb(index)}>
                    <Text style={[
                    driveStyles.breadcrumbText,
                    index === folderPath.length - 1 && driveStyles.breadcrumbActive
                    ]} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {folder.name}
                    </Text>
                </Pressable>
                </View>
            ))}
            </View>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: 50 }} />
          ) : (
            <FlatList
              data={displayItems}
              keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
              renderItem={({ item }) => (
                <DriveListItem
                  item={{ ...item, displayDate: formatLastAccessed(item.lastAccessed) }}
                  onPress={openItem}
                  onDelete={handleDeleteItem}
                />
              )}
              contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}
              ListEmptyComponent={
                <View style={styles.emptyContainer}>
                  <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={80} color={colors.textTertiary} />
                  <Text style={[driveStyles.emptyText, { color: colors.textTertiary }]}>
                      {currentFolderId ? "This folder is empty" : "No recent files"}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              }
              onRefresh={loadRecent}
              refreshing={loading}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            />
          )}
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>

      <FilePreviewModal
        open={previewOpen}
        fileId={selectedId}
        onClose={() => setPreviewOpen(false)}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import {
  View, StyleSheet, SafeAreaView, TouchableOpacity, Text, FlatList,
  ActivityIndicator, StatusBar, Pressable, Alert, Animated
} from "react-native";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { useAuth } from "../providers/AuthContext";
import { getFiles, moveToTrash } from "../../features/drive/services/fileService";
import { formatBytes } from "../../shared_code/utils/formatBytes";

// ג… ׳™׳™׳‘׳•׳ Theme
import { getDriveStyles } from "../../features/drive/styles/myDrive.styles";
import { useTheme } from "../providers/ThemeContext";

import { Swipeable } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import { subscribeDriveRefresh, emitDriveRefresh } from "../../features/drive/services/driveEvents";
import FilePreviewModal from "../../features/drive/components/FilePreviewModal";

export default function StoragePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { token } = useAuth();
  
  // ג… Theme Hooks
  const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme();
  const driveStyles = useMemo(() => getDriveStyles(colors), [colors]);

  const [files, setFiles] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null);

  const loadFiles = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!token) return;
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const data = await getFiles(token);
      setFiles(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to load files for storage view", err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [token]);

  useEffect(() => { loadFiles(); }, [loadFiles]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeDriveRefresh(() => loadFiles());
    return unsubscribe;
  }, [loadFiles]);

  // ג… ׳׳™׳•׳ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳’׳•׳“׳ (׳׳”׳’׳“׳•׳ ׳׳§׳˜׳) - ׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳§׳‘׳¦׳™׳
  const sortedFiles = useMemo(() => {
    return [...(files || [])]
      .filter((f) => f?.type === "file")
      .sort((a, b) => (b.sizeBytes ?? 0) - (a.sizeBytes ?? 0));
  }, [files]);

  const closeScreen = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      if (typeof router.canGoBack === "function" && router.canGoBack()) {
        router.back();
      } else { router.replace("/home"); }
    } catch { router.replace("/home"); }
  }, [router]);

  const openItem = useCallback((item) => {
    if (!item) return;
    // ׳‘-Storage ׳׳ ׳—׳ ׳• ׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳§׳‘׳¦׳™׳, ׳׳– ׳׳™׳ ׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ׳׳×׳™׳§׳™׳•׳×
    if (item.type === "folder") return;

    setSelectedId(item.id || item._id);
    setPreviewOpen(true);
  }, []);

  const handleDeleteItem = useCallback(async (item) => {
    Alert.alert("Move to Trash?", `Remove "${item.name}"?`, [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      {
        text: "Delete", style: "destructive",
        onPress: async () => {
          try { await moveToTrash(token, item.id); emitDriveRefresh(); } 
          catch (err) { Alert.alert("Error", "Could not delete item."); }
        }
      }
    ]);
  }, [token]);

  return (
    // ג… ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™
    <View style={[styles.modalContainer, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={isDarkMode ? "light-content" : "dark-content"} />
      <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
          <View style={styles.headerLeftSpace} />
          <TouchableOpacity 
            onPress={closeScreen} 
            style={[styles.closeButton, { backgroundColor: colors.surface }]} 
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <Ionicons name="close-outline" size={32} color={colors.textSecondary} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.content}>
          <View style={styles.textHeader}>
            <Text style={[driveStyles.homeTitle, { marginLeft: 0 }]}>STORAGE</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.subTitle, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Files using storage (descending)</Text>
          </View>

          {loading ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: 50 }} />
          ) : (
            <FlatList
              data={sortedFiles}
              keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
              renderItem={({ item }) => (
                <StorageListItem
                  item={item}
                  onPress={openItem}
                  onDelete={handleDeleteItem}
                />
              )}
              contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
              ListEmptyComponent={
                <View style={styles.emptyContainer}>
                  <Ionicons name="cloud-done-outline" size={80} color={colors.textTertiary} />
                  <Text style={[driveStyles.emptyText, { color: colors.textTertiary }]}>No files taking up storage.</Text>
                </View>
              }
              onRefresh={loadFiles}
              refreshing={loading}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            />
          )}
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>

      <FilePreviewModal
        open={previewOpen}
        fileId={selectedId}
        onClose={() => setPreviewOpen(false)}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

// ג… ׳¨׳›׳™׳‘ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳׳™ ׳׳¢׳•׳“׳›׳ ׳¢׳ ׳¦׳‘׳¢׳™׳ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™׳™׳
function StorageListItem({ item, onPress, onDelete }) {
  const swipeableRef = useRef(null);
  const { colors } = useTheme(); 
  
  const isImage = item.mimeType?.startsWith("image");
  const iconName = isImage ? "image" : "document-text";
  const iconColor = isImage ? colors.danger : colors.primary;

  const renderLeftActions = (progress, dragX) => {
    const scale = dragX.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 80], outputRange: [0.8, 1], extrapolate: 'clamp' });
    const opacity = dragX.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 50], outputRange: [0, 1], extrapolate: 'clamp' });

    return (
      <View style={styles.leftActionContainer}>
        <Animated.View style={[styles.deleteActionAnim, { backgroundColor: colors.danger, opacity, transform: [{ scale }] }]}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              activeOpacity={0.7}
              style={styles.deleteButtonInner}
              onPress={() => {
                swipeableRef.current?.close();
                setTimeout(() => { if (onDelete) onDelete(item); }, 200);
              }}
            >
              <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={24} color="white" />
              <Text style={styles.deleteText}>Delete</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
        </Animated.View>
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <Swipeable
      ref={swipeableRef}
      renderLeftActions={renderLeftActions}
      overshootLeft={false}
      friction={2}
    >
      <Pressable 
        onPress={() => onPress(item)} 
        // ג… ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳‘׳•׳¨׳“׳¨ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™׳™׳
        style={[styles.fileRow, { borderBottomColor: colors.border, backgroundColor: colors.card }]}
      >
        <View style={styles.iconWrapper}>
          <Ionicons name={iconName} size={24} color={iconColor} />
        </View>
        <View style={styles.fileInfo}>
          <Text style={[styles.fileName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {item.name}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[styles.fileSize, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{formatBytes(item.sizeBytes)}</Text>
        </View>
      </Pressable>
    </Swipeable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  modalContainer: { flex: 1 },
  modalHeader: {
    flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 15, height: 60,
  },
  headerLeftSpace: { width: 40 },
  closeButton: {
    width: 44, height: 44, justifyContent: "center", alignItems: "center", borderRadius: 22,
  },
  content: { flex: 1 },
  textHeader: { paddingHorizontal: 20, marginBottom: 15 },
  subTitle: { fontSize: 13, marginTop: -8, marginLeft: 0 },
  listContent: { paddingHorizontal: 20, paddingBottom: 20 },

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View,

  SafeAreaView,

  StatusBar,
} from "react-native";

import {
  getTrashFiles,
  restoreFromTrash,
  deleteForever,
  emptyTrash,
  getDaysLeft,
} from "../../features/drive/services/fileService";
import DriveListItem from "../../features/drive/components/DriveListItem";

// ג… Theme Imports
import { getDriveStyles } from "../../features/drive/styles/myDrive.styles";
import { useTheme } from "../providers/ThemeContext";

import { subscribeDriveRefresh, emitDriveRefresh } from "../../features/drive/services/driveEvents";

export default function TrashPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { token } = useAuth();
  
  // ג… Theme Hooks
  const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme();
  const driveStyles = useMemo(() => getDriveStyles(colors), [colors]);

  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const loadTrash = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!token) return;
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const data = await getTrashFiles(token);
      setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to load trash:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [token]);

  useEffect(() => { loadTrash(); }, [loadTrash]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeDriveRefresh(() => loadTrash());
    return unsubscribe;
  }, [loadTrash]);

  const closeScreen = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      if (typeof router.canGoBack === "function" && router.canGoBack()) {
        router.back();
      } else { router.replace("/home"); }
    } catch { router.replace("/home"); }
  }, [router]);

  const handleEmptyTrash = () => {
    Alert.alert(
      "Empty Trash?",
      "All items in the trash will be permanently deleted. This action cannot be undone.",
      [
        { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
        {
          text: "Empty Trash",
          style: "destructive",
          onPress: async () => {
            await emptyTrash(token);
            emitDriveRefresh();
          },
        },
      ]
    );
  };

  const handleDeleteForever = useCallback(async (item) => {
    Alert.alert(
      "Delete Forever?",
      `"${item.name}" will be gone forever.`,
      [
        { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
        {
          text: "Delete Forever",
          style: "destructive",
          onPress: async () => {
             try {
                 await deleteForever(token, item.id);
                 emitDriveRefresh();
             } catch (err) {
                 Alert.alert("Error", "Could not delete item.");
             }
          }
        }
      ]
    );
  }, [token]);

  const handleRestore = useCallback(async (item) => {
    if (!item) return;
    try {
      await restoreFromTrash(token, item.id);
      emitDriveRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Could not restore item.");
    }
  }, [token]);

  return (
    // ג… ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™
    <View style={[styles.modalContainer, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={isDarkMode ? "light-content" : "dark-content"} />
      <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={handleEmptyTrash}
            disabled={items.length === 0}
            style={{ paddingLeft: 5 }}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.emptyAction, items.length === 0 && { opacity: 0.3 }]}>
              Empty
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity 
            onPress={closeScreen} 
            style={[styles.closeButton, { backgroundColor: colors.surface }]} 
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            
            <Ionicons name="close-outline" size={32} color={colors.textSecondary} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.content}>
          <View style={styles.textHeader}>
            <Text style={[driveStyles.homeTitle, { marginLeft: 0 }]}>TRASH</Text>

            {/* ג… ׳‘׳׳ ׳¨ ׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳׳•׳×׳׳ ׳¦׳‘׳¢׳™׳ */}
            <View style={[styles.infoBanner, { backgroundColor: colors.surface }]}>
              <Ionicons name="information-circle" size={16} color={colors.primary} />
              <Text style={[styles.infoText, { color: colors.primary }]}>Items are deleted forever after 30 days</Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {loading ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: 50 }} />
          ) : (

              data={items}
              keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}

                  onRestore={handleRestore}

                />

              contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}

                <View style={styles.emptyContainer}>
                  <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={80} color={colors.textTertiary} />
                  <Text style={[driveStyles.emptyText, { color: colors.textTertiary }]}>Trash is empty</Text>
                </View>
              }
              onRefresh={loadTrash}
              refreshing={loading}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            />
          )}
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    </View>
  );
}

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage'; 
import { useRouter, useSegments } from "expo-router";

// ג… ׳•׳•׳“׳ ׳©׳”׳ ׳×׳™׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳׳׳• ׳ ׳›׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳¦׳׳ ׳‘׳₪׳¨׳•׳™׳§׳˜
import { authenticateUser } from "../../features/auth/services/authService";
import { getUserProfile } from "../../features/user/services/userService";

const TOKEN_KEY = "token";
const USER_KEY = "user_data";
const AuthContext = createContext(null);

function getUserIdFromToken(token) {
  try {
    const base64Url = token.split('.')[1];
    const base64 = base64Url.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    const jsonPayload = decodeURIComponent(atob(base64).split('').map(function(c) {
        return '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2);
    }).join(''));
    return JSON.parse(jsonPayload).userId;
  } catch (e) {
    try {
        const payload = JSON.parse(String(Buffer.from(token.split('.')[1], 'base64')));
        return payload.userId;
    } catch (err) {
        return null; 
    }
  }
}

// Polyfill for atob
if (!global.atob) {
    global.atob = (input) => {
        const chars = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/=';
        let str = input.replace(/=+$/, '');
        let output = '';
        if (str.length % 4 == 1) throw new Error("'atob' failed");
        for (let bc = 0, bs = 0, buffer, i = 0; buffer = str.charAt(i++); ~buffer && (bs = bc % 4 ? bs * 64 + buffer : buffer, bc++ % 4) ? output += String.fromCharCode(255 & bs >> (-2 * bc & 6)) : 0) {
            buffer = chars.indexOf(buffer);
        }
        return output;
    }
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [token, setToken] = useState(null);
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [isHydrating, setIsHydrating] = useState(true);
  const router = useRouter();
  const segments = useSegments();

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    (async () => {
      try {
        const savedToken = await AsyncStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
        const savedUser = await AsyncStorage.getItem(USER_KEY);
        
        if (savedToken && isMounted) {
            setToken(savedToken);
            if (savedUser) setUser(JSON.parse(savedUser));
            
            // ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳× ׳₪׳¨׳•׳₪׳™׳ ׳‘׳¨׳§׳¢
            const uid = getUserIdFromToken(savedToken);
            if (uid) {
                getUserProfile(savedToken, uid).then(userData => {
                    if (userData && isMounted) {
                        setUser(userData);
                        AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(userData));
                    }
                }).catch(() => {});
            }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Hydration error", e);
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) setIsHydrating(false);
      }
    })();

    return () => { isMounted = false; };
  }, []);

  // ג… ׳”׳’׳ ׳” ׳¢׳ ׳”׳ ׳™׳•׳•׳˜: ׳׳ ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳׳ ׳׳—׳•׳‘׳¨ ׳•׳”׳•׳ ׳‘׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳׳₪׳׳™׳§׳¦׳™׳”, ׳–׳¨׳•׳§ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳׳׳•׳’׳™׳
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isHydrating) return;

    const inAuthGroup = segments[0] === '(auth)';
    
    if (!token && !inAuthGroup) {
        // ׳׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳§׳ ׳•׳׳ ׳ ׳׳¦׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳§׳‘׳•׳¦׳× auth -> ׳׳ ׳׳׳•׳’׳™׳
        router.replace('/(auth)/login');
    } else if (token && inAuthGroup) {
        // ׳׳ ׳™׳© ׳˜׳•׳§׳ ׳•׳ ׳׳¦׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳§׳‘׳•׳¦׳× auth -> ׳׳ ׳׳‘׳™׳×
        router.replace('/(app)/home');
    }
  }, [token, isHydrating, segments]);

  const login = async (email, password) => {
    try {
      const res = await authenticateUser(email, password);
      if (!res.success) return { ok: false, error: res.error };

      const newToken = res.token;
      const userId = getUserIdFromToken(newToken); 
      
      let userData = null;
      if (userId) {
         try { userData = await getUserProfile(newToken, userId); } catch (err) {}
      }

      await AsyncStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, newToken);
      if (userData) await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(userData));

      setToken(newToken);
      setUser(userData);
      
      // ׳”׳ ׳™׳•׳•׳˜ ׳™׳×׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳׳•׳˜׳•׳׳˜׳™׳× ׳‘-useEffect ׳׳׳¢׳׳”
      return { ok: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: "Login failed unexpected" };
    }
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    await AsyncStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    await AsyncStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
    setToken(null);
    setUser(null);
  };

  const value = useMemo(() => ({
    token, user, isAuthenticated: !!token, isHydrating, login, logout
  }), [token, user, isHydrating]);

  // ג… ׳”׳×׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳”׳§׳¨׳™׳˜׳™: ׳׳ ׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳₪׳׳™׳§׳¦׳™׳” ׳¢׳“ ׳©׳¡׳™׳™׳׳ ׳• ׳׳˜׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳˜׳•׳§׳
  // ׳–׳” ׳׳•׳ ׳¢ ׳׳× ׳”׳§׳₪׳™׳¦׳” ׳׳׳¡׳ ׳”׳׳•׳’׳™׳ ׳›׳©׳™׳© ׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳׳—׳•׳‘׳¨
  if (isHydrating) {
      return null; // ׳׳• ׳©׳™׳ ׳›׳׳ ׳¨׳›׳™׳‘ <SplashScreen />
  }

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext);
export default AuthProvider;

import React, { createContext, useState, useContext, useEffect } from "react";
import { Audio } from "expo-av";

// ׳•׳•׳“׳ ׳©׳”׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ ׳ ׳›׳•׳ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳׳” ׳©׳›׳×׳‘׳×
import songFile from "../../assets/videos/Song.mp3"; 

const MusicContext = createContext();

export const useMusic = () => useContext(MusicContext);

export const MusicProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const [sound, setSound] = useState();
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳•׳× ׳¡׳׳•׳ ׳“ (׳›׳“׳™ ׳©׳™׳₪׳¢׳ ׳’׳ ׳‘׳׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳§׳˜ ׳‘׳׳™׳™׳₪׳•׳)
    async function configureAudio() {
      try {
        await Audio.setAudioModeAsync({
          playsInSilentModeIOS: true,
          staysActiveInBackground: true,
          shouldDuckAndroid: true,
          playThroughEarpieceAndroid: false,
        });
        await loadSound();
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Audio setup failed", e);
      }
    }
    configureAudio();

    return () => {
      // ׳ ׳™׳§׳•׳™ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳™׳׳” ׳׳”׳׳₪׳׳™׳§׳¦׳™׳” ׳׳’׳׳¨׳™
      if (sound) {
        sound.unloadAsync();
      }
    };
  }, []);

  async function loadSound() {
    const { sound: newSound } = await Audio.Sound.createAsync(
       songFile,
       { isLooping: true } // ׳©׳”׳©׳™׳¨ ׳™׳—׳–׳•׳¨ ׳¢׳ ׳¢׳¦׳׳•
    );
    setSound(newSound);
  }

  const toggleMusic = async () => {
    if (!sound) return;

    if (isPlaying) {
      await sound.pauseAsync();
      setIsPlaying(false);
    } else {
      await sound.playAsync();
      setIsPlaying(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <MusicContext.Provider value={{ isPlaying, toggleMusic }}>
      {children}
    </MusicContext.Provider>
  );
};
export default MusicProvider;
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\app\providers\ThemeContext.jsx =====
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import { useColorScheme } from "react-native"; 
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { theme } from "../../shared_code/styles/theme";

const ThemeContext = createContext();
const THEME_KEY = "app_theme_mode";

export const ThemeProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const systemScheme = useColorScheme(); 
  // ׳׳×׳—׳™׳׳™׳ ׳‘-false ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳׳ ׳•׳¢ ׳”׳‘׳”׳•׳‘ ׳¢׳“ ׳©׳”׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳” ׳׳¡׳×׳™׳™׳׳×, ׳׳• ׳׳₪׳™ ׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×
  const [isDarkMode, setIsDarkMode] = useState(systemScheme === "dark");
  const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

  // ׳˜׳¢׳™׳ ׳× ׳”׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳” ׳”׳©׳׳•׳¨׳” ׳‘׳¢׳× ׳¢׳׳™׳™׳× ׳”׳׳₪׳׳™׳§׳¦׳™׳”
  useEffect(() => {
    const loadTheme = async () => {
      try {
        const savedTheme = await AsyncStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY);
        if (savedTheme !== null) {
          setIsDarkMode(savedTheme === "dark");
        } else {
            // ׳׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳” ׳©׳׳•׳¨׳”, ׳”׳•׳׳›׳™׳ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳•׳× ׳”׳˜׳׳₪׳•׳
            setIsDarkMode(systemScheme === "dark");
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.log("Failed to load theme", e);
      } finally {
        setIsLoaded(true);
      }
    };
    loadTheme();
  }, []);

  const colors = useMemo(() => {
    return isDarkMode ? theme.dark : theme.light;
  }, [isDarkMode]);

  const toggleTheme = async () => {
    const newMode = !isDarkMode;
    setIsDarkMode(newMode);
    try {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, newMode ? "dark" : "light");
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to save theme", e);
    }
  };

  // ׳׳•׳ ׳¢ ׳¨׳™׳¦׳•׳“ ׳¨׳׳©׳•׳ ׳™ ׳©׳ ׳¦׳‘׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳ ׳›׳•׳ ׳™׳
  if (!isLoaded) return null;

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ isDarkMode, toggleTheme, colors }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useTheme = () => {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useTheme must be used within a ThemeProvider");
  }
  return context;
};
export default ThemeProvider;

          <TextInput
            value={email}
            onChangeText={setEmail}
            autoCapitalize="none"
            keyboardType="email-address"
            placeholder="username@copa.com"
            placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary} // ׳¦׳‘׳¢ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™
            style={styles.input}
          />
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.label}>Password</Text>
        <View style={styles.inputWrapper}>
          <Ionicons name="lock-closed-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary} style={styles.inputIcon} />
          <TextInput
            value={password}
            onChangeText={setPassword}
            secureTextEntry
            placeholder="ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢"
            placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
            style={styles.input}
          />
        </View>

          disabled={!canSubmit}
          onPress={onLogin}
          style={[styles.btnPrimary, !canSubmit && styles.btnDisabled]}
        >

            <Text style={styles.btnTextPrimary}>Sign In</Text>

import React from "react";
import { View, Text, TextInput, StyleSheet, Keyboard, Image, Pressable, Alert } from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';

// ג… ׳׳§׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳׳× styles ׳•-colors
export default function Step2({ formData, setFormData, errors, styles, colors }) {

  const pickImage = async () => {
    const { status } = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();

    if (status !== 'granted') {
      Alert.alert("Permission Denied", "We need permission to access your photos.");
      return;
    }

    const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
      allowsEditing: true,
      aspect: [1, 1],
      quality: 0.7,
    });

    if (!result.canceled) {
      setFormData({ ...formData, image: result.assets[0].uri });
    }
  };

  const handlePhoneChange = (text) => {
    let cleaned = text.replace(/\D/g, "");
    if (cleaned.length > 10) cleaned = cleaned.slice(0, 10);

    let formatted = cleaned;
    if (cleaned.length > 3 && cleaned.length <= 6) {
      formatted = `${cleaned.slice(0, 3)}-${cleaned.slice(3)}`;
    } else if (cleaned.length > 6) {
      formatted = `${cleaned.slice(0, 3)}-${cleaned.slice(3, 6)}-${cleaned.slice(6)}`;
    }

    setFormData({ ...formData, phoneNumber: formatted });
  };

  return (
    <View>
      <View style={localStyles.imageContainer}>
        <Pressable onPress={pickImage} style={[localStyles.avatarPlaceholder, { borderColor: colors.border }]}>
          {formData.image ? (
            <Image source={{ uri: formData.image }} style={localStyles.avatarImage} />
          ) : (
            <Ionicons name="image-outline" size={40} color={colors.textTertiary} />
          )}
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={[localStyles.imageText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
          {formData.image ? "Tap to change photo" : "Tap to upload photo"}
        </Text>
        {errors.image && <Text style={styles.errorText}>{errors.image}</Text>}
      </View>

      <Text style={styles.label}>Phone Number</Text>
      <View style={styles.inputWrapper}>
        <Ionicons name="call-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary} style={styles.inputIcon} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          placeholder="05X-XXX-XXXX"
          placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
          keyboardType="phone-pad"
          value={formData.phoneNumber}
          onChangeText={handlePhoneChange}
          returnKeyType="done"
          onSubmitEditing={Keyboard.dismiss}
        />
      </View>
      <Text style={{ fontSize: 12, color: colors.textTertiary, marginBottom: 5, marginLeft: 4 }}>
        Israeli mobile numbers only
      </Text>

      {errors.phoneNumber && <Text style={styles.errorText}>{errors.phoneNumber}</Text>}
    </View>
  );
}

const localStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  imageContainer: { alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 20 },
  avatarPlaceholder: {
    width: 100,
    height: 100,
    borderRadius: 50,
    backgroundColor: "rgba(0,0,0,0.05)", // ׳©׳§׳•׳£ ׳¢׳“׳™׳ ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳”׳×׳׳™׳ ׳׳›׳”׳”/׳‘׳”׳™׳¨
    justifyContent: "center",

    overflow: "hidden"
  },
  avatarImage: {
    width: "100%",
    height: "100%"
  },
  imageText: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: "500" },
});

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { View, Text, Pressable, Platform } from "react-native";
import DateTimePicker from "@react-native-community/datetimepicker";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

// ג… ׳”׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳” ׳׳§׳‘׳׳× authStyles
export default function Step3({ formData, setFormData, errors, authStyles, colors }) {
  const [showDatePicker, setShowDatePicker] = useState(false);

  const getDateValue = () => {
    if (formData.dateOfBirth) {
      const parsed = new Date(formData.dateOfBirth);
      if (!isNaN(parsed)) return parsed;
    }
    const defaultDate = new Date();
    defaultDate.setFullYear(defaultDate.getFullYear() - 18);
    return defaultDate;
  };

  const handleDateChange = (event, selectedDate) => {
    if (Platform.OS === "android") {
      setShowDatePicker(false);
    }
    if (event.type === "set" && selectedDate) {
      const year = selectedDate.getFullYear();
      const month = String(selectedDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
      const day = String(selectedDate.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
      setFormData({ ...formData, dateOfBirth: `${year}-${month}-${day}` });
    }
  };

  const formatDisplayDate = (dateStr) => {
    if (!dateStr) return "Select your birthday";
    const date = new Date(dateStr);
    if (isNaN(date)) return "Select your birthday";
    return date.toLocaleDateString("en-US", {
      year: "numeric",
      month: "long",
      day: "numeric",
    });
  };

  return (
    <View>
      {/* ג… ׳×׳•׳§׳ ׳-authStyles */}
      <Text style={authStyles.label}>Date of Birth</Text>
      
      <Pressable
        style={[
          authStyles.dateButton || authStyles.inputWrapper, 
          errors.dateOfBirth && authStyles.dateButtonError
        ]}
        onPress={() => setShowDatePicker(true)}
      >
        <Ionicons name="calendar-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary} style={{ marginRight: 10 }} />
        <Text style={[
          authStyles.dateText,
          !formData.dateOfBirth && authStyles.dateTextPlaceholder
        ]}>
          {formatDisplayDate(formData.dateOfBirth)}
        </Text>
      </Pressable>
      
      {/* ג… ׳×׳•׳§׳ ׳-authStyles */}
      {errors.dateOfBirth && <Text style={authStyles.errorText}>{errors.dateOfBirth}</Text>}

      {showDatePicker && (
        <DateTimePicker
          value={getDateValue()}
          mode="date"
          display={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "spinner" : "default"}
          onChange={handleDateChange}
          maximumDate={new Date()}
          minimumDate={new Date(1900, 0, 1)}
          themeVariant={colors.background === "#121212" ? "dark" : "light"}
        />
      )}

      {/* ג… ׳×׳•׳§׳ ׳-authStyles */}
      <Text style={[authStyles.label, { marginTop: 10 }]}>Gender</Text>

      <View style={authStyles.genderContainer}>
        <View style={authStyles.genderRow}>
          {[{ label: "Male", value: "male" }, { label: "Female", value: "female" }, { label: "Other", value: "other" }].map((g) => (
            <Pressable
              key={g.value}
              style={[
                authStyles.genderBtn,
                formData.gender === g.value && authStyles.genderBtnActive
              ]}
              onPress={() => setFormData({ ...formData, gender: g.value })}
            >
              <Text style={[
                authStyles.genderText,
                formData.gender === g.value && authStyles.genderTextActive
              ]}>
                {g.label}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          ))}
        </View>

        <Pressable
          style={[
            authStyles.genderBtnFull, 
            formData.gender === "prefer_not_to_say" && authStyles.genderBtnActive
          ]}
          onPress={() => setFormData({ ...formData, gender: "prefer_not_to_say" })}
        >
          <Text style={[
            authStyles.genderText,
            formData.gender === "prefer_not_to_say" && authStyles.genderTextActive
          ]}>
            Prefer not to say
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      {/* ג… ׳×׳•׳§׳ ׳-authStyles */}
      {errors.gender && <Text style={authStyles.errorText}>{errors.gender}</Text>}
    </View>
  );
}
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\auth\pages\Register\steps\Step4.jsx =====
import React from "react";
import { View, Text, TextInput } from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

export default function Step4({ formData, setFormData, errors, styles, colors }) {
  
  const handleEmailChange = (text) => {
    let val = text;
    if (val.endsWith("@") && val.length > (formData.email || "").length) {
      if ((val.match(/@/g) || []).length === 1) {
        val += "copa.com";
      }
    }
    setFormData({ ...formData, email: val });
  };

  return (
    <View>
      <Text style={styles.label}>Email</Text>
      <View style={styles.inputWrapper}>
        <Ionicons name="mail-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary} style={styles.inputIcon} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          placeholder="username@copa.com"
          placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
          keyboardType="email-address"
          autoCapitalize="none"
          value={formData.email}
          onChangeText={handleEmailChange}
        />
      </View>
      {errors.email && <Text style={styles.errorText}>{errors.email}</Text>}

      <Text style={styles.label}>Password</Text>
      <View style={styles.inputWrapper}>
        <Ionicons name="lock-closed-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary} style={styles.inputIcon} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          placeholder="ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢"
          placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
          secureTextEntry
          value={formData.password}
          onChangeText={(text) => setFormData({ ...formData, password: text })}
        />
      </View>
      {errors.password && <Text style={styles.errorText}>{errors.password}</Text>}

      <Text style={styles.label}>Confirm Password</Text>
      <View style={styles.inputWrapper}>
        <Ionicons name="lock-closed-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary} style={styles.inputIcon} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          placeholder="ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢ג€¢"
          placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
          secureTextEntry
          value={formData.verifyPassword}
          onChangeText={(text) => setFormData({ ...formData, verifyPassword: text })}
        />
      </View>
      {errors.verifyPassword && <Text style={styles.errorText}>{errors.verifyPassword}</Text>}
    </View>
  );
}

===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\auth\pages\Register\RegisterScreen.jsx =====
import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { View, Text, Pressable, ActivityIndicator, Alert, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import { useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { registerUser } from "../../services/authService";
import AuthLayout from "../../components/AuthLayout";

import Step1 from "./steps/Step1";
import Step2 from "./steps/Step2";
import Step3 from "./steps/Step3";
import Step4 from "./steps/Step4";
import Step5 from "./steps/Step5";

import {
  validateStep1, validateStep2, validateStep3, validateStep4
} from "../../validators/registerValidators";

import { useTheme } from "../../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";
import { getAuthStyles } from "../../styles/auth.styles";

const TOTAL_STEPS = 4;

export default function RegisterScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  
  const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme();
  const styles = useMemo(() => getAuthStyles(colors, isDarkMode), [colors, isDarkMode]);

  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    firstName: "",
    lastName: "",
    phoneNumber: "",
    image: null,
    dateOfBirth: "",
    gender: "",
    email: "",
    password: "",
    verifyPassword: "",
  });

  const getHeaderTitle = () => {
    switch (step) {
      case 1: return "Who are you?";
      case 2: return "Contact Info";
      case 3: return "Personal Info";
      case 4: return "Security";
      case 5: return "Welcome!";
      default: return "";
    }
  };

  const validateCurrentStep = () => {
    let stepErrors = {};
    if (step === 1) stepErrors = validateStep1(formData);
    if (step === 2) stepErrors = validateStep2(formData);
    if (step === 3) stepErrors = validateStep3(formData);
    if (step === 4) stepErrors = validateStep4(formData);

    setErrors(stepErrors);
    return Object.keys(stepErrors).length === 0;
  };

  const handleNext = async () => {
    if (!validateCurrentStep()) return;
    if (step < TOTAL_STEPS) setStep(step + 1);
    else await handleSubmit();
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    if (step > 1 && step <= TOTAL_STEPS) {
      setStep(step - 1);
      setErrors({});
    } else {
      router.replace("/login");
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const payload = {
      firstName: String(formData.firstName || "").trim(),
      lastName: String(formData.lastName || "").trim(),
      phoneNumber: String(formData.phoneNumber || "").trim(),
      dateOfBirth: String(formData.dateOfBirth || "").trim(),
      gender: String(formData.gender || "").trim(),
      email: String(formData.email || "").trim().toLowerCase(),
      password: String(formData.password || ""),
    };
    if (formData.image) payload.image = String(formData.image);

    const res = await registerUser(payload);
    setLoading(false);

    if (res?.success) setStep(5);
    else Alert.alert("Registration Failed", res?.error || "Unknown error");
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout subtitle={getHeaderTitle()}>
      
      <View style={{ minHeight: 300 }}>
        {step === 1 && <Step1 formData={formData} setFormData={setFormData} errors={errors} styles={styles} colors={colors} />}
        {step === 2 && <Step2 formData={formData} setFormData={setFormData} errors={errors} styles={styles} colors={colors} />}
        
        {/* ג… ׳”׳×׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳›׳׳: ׳׳¢׳‘׳™׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳¡׳˜׳™׳™׳׳™׳ ׳×׳—׳× ׳”׳©׳ authStyles ׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨ Step3 */}
        {step === 3 && (
          <Step3 
            formData={formData} 
            setFormData={setFormData} 
            errors={errors} 
            authStyles={styles} 
            colors={colors} 
          />
        )}
        
        {step === 4 && <Step4 formData={formData} setFormData={setFormData} errors={errors} styles={styles} colors={colors} />}
        {step === 5 && <Step5 styles={styles} />}
      </View>

      <View style={{ marginTop: 20 }}>
        {step === 5 ? (
          <Pressable style={styles.btnPrimary} onPress={() => router.replace("/login")}>
            <Text style={styles.btnTextPrimary}>Proceed to Login</Text>
          </Pressable>
        ) : (
          <View style={{ flexDirection: "row", gap: 15 }}>
            <Pressable style={[styles.btnSecondary, { flex: 1 }]} onPress={handleBack}>
              <Text style={styles.btnTextSecondary}>{step === 1 ? "Login" : "Back"}</Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={[styles.btnPrimary, { flex: 1, marginTop: 10 }]}
              onPress={handleNext}
              disabled={loading}
            >
              {loading ? (
                <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.btnTextPrimary}>
                  {step === TOTAL_STEPS ? "Submit" : "Next"}
                </Text>
              )}
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>

      {step <= TOTAL_STEPS && (
        <View style={localStyles.dots}>
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
            <View key={i} style={[localStyles.dot, i === step && styles.btnPrimary?.backgroundColor ? { backgroundColor: styles.btnPrimary.backgroundColor, width: 20 } : localStyles.dotActive]} />
          ))}
        </View>
      )}
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}

const localStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  dots: { flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "center", gap: 8, marginTop: 25 },
  dot: { width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: "#ddd" },
  dotActive: { backgroundColor: "#007bff", width: 20 },
});
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\auth\services\authService.js =====
import { request } from "../../../shared_code/hooks/api/request";
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

const isWeb = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof localStorage !== 'undefined';

// Login User
export const authenticateUser = async (email, password) => {
  try {
    const data = await request("/api/tokens", {
      method: "POST",
      body: { email, password },
    });
    
    return { success: true, token: data.token, error: null };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Login error:", error);
    return { success: false, error: error.message || "Login failed" };
  }
};

// Register User
export const registerUser = async (userData) => {
  try {
    const data = await request("/api/users", {
      method: "POST",
      body: userData,
    });

    return { success: true, data };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Registration error:", error);
    return { success: false, error: error.message || "Registration failed" };
  }
};

// Storage Helpers
export const saveToken = async (token) => {
  if (isWeb) localStorage.setItem('token', token);
  else await AsyncStorage.setItem('token', token);
};

export const removeToken = async () => {
  if (isWeb) localStorage.removeItem('token');
  else await AsyncStorage.removeItem('token');
};

export const getToken = async () => {
  if (isWeb) return localStorage.getItem('token');
  return await AsyncStorage.getItem('token');
};

export const validateStep1 = (data) => {
  const errors = {};
  if (!data.firstName || data.firstName.trim().length < 2) {
    errors.firstName = "First name is required (min 2 chars)";
  }
  return errors;
};

export const validateStep2 = (data) => {
  const errors = {};
  if (data.phoneNumber && !/^05\d-?\d{3}-?\d{4}$/.test(data.phoneNumber)) {
    errors.phoneNumber = "Invalid Israeli phone number";
  }
  return errors;
};

export const validateStep3 = (data) => {
  const errors = {};
  if (!data.dateOfBirth) errors.dateOfBirth = "Date of birth is required";
  if (!data.gender) errors.gender = "Gender is required";
  return errors;
};

export const validateStep4 = (data) => {
  const errors = {};
  if (!data.email || !data.email.includes("@")) {
    errors.email = "Invalid email address";
  } else if (!data.email.endsWith("@copa.com")) {
    errors.email = "Email must end with @copa.com";
  }

  if (!data.password || data.password.length < 8) {
    errors.password = "Password must be at least 8 chars";
  }

  if (data.password !== data.verifyPassword) {
    errors.verifyPassword = "Passwords do not match";
  }

  return errors;
};

import React, { useRef, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  Pressable,
  Modal,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Alert,
  Dimensions,
  Animated,
  StyleSheet
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

import { getDriveStyles } from "../styles/myDrive.styles";
import { getDriveListItemStyles } from "../styles/driveListItem.styles";
import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";

import { getFileIconMeta } from "../utils/fileIcon";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";
import { Swipeable } from "react-native-gesture-handler";

import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { emitDriveRefresh } from "../services/driveEvents";

  setStarred,
  setImportant,
  getFileById,
} from "../services/fileService";

export default function DriveListItem({ item, onPress, onDelete, onRestore, trashMode = false }) {
  const isFolder = item.type === "folder";
  const { icon, color } = getFileIconMeta(item.name, item.mimeType, isFolder);

  const { token, user } = useAuth();
  const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme();
  const driveStyles = useMemo(() => getDriveStyles(colors), [colors]);
  const styles = useMemo(() => getDriveListItemStyles(colors), [colors]);

  const swipeableRef = useRef(null);
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [menuPos, setMenuPos] = useState({ top: 0, left: 0 });
  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = useState(false);
  const [shareOpen, setShareOpen] = useState(false);

  const dotsRef = useRef(null);
  const isStarred = !!item.starred;
  const isImportant = !!item.important;

  // ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳× ׳‘׳¢׳׳•׳×
  const isOwner = useMemo(() => {
    const ownerId = item?.ownerId || item?.owner;
    const myId = user?.id || user?._id;
    return String(ownerId) === String(myId);
  }, [item, user]);

  const closeMenu = () => setMenuOpen(false);

  const renderLeftActions = (progress, dragX) => {
    const scale = dragX.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 80], outputRange: [0.8, 1], extrapolate: 'clamp' });
    const opacity = dragX.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 50], outputRange: [0, 1], extrapolate: 'clamp' });

    // ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳¡׳ ׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨: ׳”׳—׳׳§׳” ׳׳©׳—׳–׳•׳¨
    if (trashMode) {
      return (
        <View style={[styles.leftActionContainer, { backgroundColor: '#22c55e' }]}>
          <Animated.View style={[styles.deleteActionAnim, { opacity, transform: [{ scale }] }]}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              activeOpacity={0.7}
              style={[styles.deleteButtonInner, { backgroundColor: '#22c55e' }]}
              onPress={() => {
                swipeableRef.current?.close();
                setTimeout(() => { if (onRestore) onRestore(item); }, 200);
              }}
            >
              <Ionicons name="refresh-outline" size={24} color="white" />
              <Text style={styles.deleteText}>Restore</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </Animated.View>
        </View>
      );
    }

    // ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳¨׳’׳™׳: ׳”׳—׳׳§׳” ׳׳׳—׳™׳§׳” (׳¨׳§ ׳׳‘׳¢׳׳™׳!)
    return (
      <View style={styles.leftActionContainer}>
        <Animated.View style={[styles.deleteActionAnim, { opacity, transform: [{ scale }] }]}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.7}
            style={styles.deleteButtonInner}
            onPress={() => {
              swipeableRef.current?.close();
              setTimeout(() => { if (onDelete) onDelete(item); }, 200);
            }}
          >
            <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={24} color="white" />
            <Text style={styles.deleteText}>Delete</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </Animated.View>
      </View>
    );
  };

  const handleDownload = async () => {
    closeMenu();
    if (!token) return;
    try {
      const full = await getFileById(token, item.id);
      if (!full || !full.content) return;

      await downloadFile({
        content: full.content,
        mimeType: full.mimeType || "application/octet-stream",
        fileName: full.name || item.name || "file",
        isBase64: !String(full.mimeType).startsWith('text/')
      });
    } catch (e) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Download failed.");
    }
  };

  const menuOptions = useMemo(() => {
    // 1. ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳׳©׳₪׳”
    if (trashMode) {
      return [
        {
          key: "restore", icon: "refresh-outline", label: "RESTORE",
          onPress: () => { closeMenu(); if (onRestore) onRestore(item); }
        },
        {
          key: "deleteForever", icon: "trash-outline", label: "DELETE FOREVER",
          onPress: () => { closeMenu(); if (onDelete) onDelete(item); }
        }
      ];
    }

    // 2. ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳¨׳’׳™׳ - ׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳× ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳” ׳‘׳¡׳™׳¡׳™׳×
    const options = [
      { key: "share", icon: "share-social-outline", label: "SHARE", onPress: () => { closeMenu(); setShareOpen(true); } },
      {
        key: "star", icon: isStarred ? "star" : "star-outline", label: isStarred ? "REMOVE STAR" : "ADD TO STAR",
        onPress: async () => { if (!token) return; closeMenu(); try { await setStarred(token, item.id, !isStarred); emitDriveRefresh(); } catch (e) { } },
      },
      {
        key: "important", icon: isImportant ? "alert-circle" : "alert-circle-outline", label: isImportant ? "REMOVE IMPORTANT" : "ADD TO IMPORTANT",
        onPress: async () => { if (!token) return; closeMenu(); try { await setImportant(token, item.id, !isImportant); emitDriveRefresh(); } catch (e) { } },
      },
      { key: "download", icon: "download-outline", label: "DOWNLOAD", onPress: handleDownload },
      {
        key: "edit", icon: "pencil-outline", label: "EDIT",
        onPress: () => { closeMenu(); setEditOpen(true); },
      },
    ];

    // 3. ׳”׳•׳¡׳₪׳× ׳׳—׳™׳§׳” ׳¨׳§ ׳׳ ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳”׳•׳ ׳”׳‘׳¢׳׳™׳
    if (isOwner) {
      options.push({
        key: "delete",
        icon: "trash-outline",
        label: "MOVE TO TRASH",
        onPress: () => { closeMenu(); if (onDelete) onDelete(item); }
      });
    }

    return options;
  }, [token, item, isStarred, isImportant, onDelete, isOwner, trashMode, onRestore]);

  const openMenu = () => {
    if (!dotsRef.current) return setMenuOpen(true);
    dotsRef.current.measureInWindow((x, y, w, h) => {
      const { height: screenH } = Dimensions.get("window");
      const MENU_W = 260;
      const anchorX = x + w / 2;
      const anchorY = y + h / 2;
      setMenuPos({ top: anchorY - 100, left: anchorX - MENU_W - 12 });
      setMenuOpen(true);
    });
  };

  return (
    <Swipeable
      ref={swipeableRef}
      // ׳”׳¡׳×׳¨׳× ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳× ׳”-Swipe ׳׳ ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳”׳‘׳¢׳׳™׳ (׳‘׳׳¦׳‘ ׳¨׳’׳™׳)
      renderLeftActions={((onDelete && isOwner) || (trashMode && onRestore)) ? renderLeftActions : undefined}
      overshootLeft={false}
      friction={2}
    >
      <Pressable style={driveStyles.fileCard} onPress={() => onPress && onPress(item)}>
        <View style={driveStyles.iconContainer}>
          <Ionicons name={icon} size={28} color={color} />
        </View>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Text style={[driveStyles.fileName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {item.name}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[driveStyles.fileInfo, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
            {isFolder ? "Folder" : "File"} ג€¢ {item.displayDate || new Date(item.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString()}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <Pressable ref={dotsRef} onPress={() => (menuOpen ? closeMenu() : openMenu())} hitSlop={14} style={{ padding: 6 }}>
          <Ionicons name="ellipsis-vertical" size={18} color={colors.textSecondary} />
        </Pressable>
      </Pressable>

      <Modal visible={menuOpen} transparent animationType="none">
        <Pressable style={styles.backdrop} onPress={closeMenu}>
          <BlurView intensity={22} tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "light"} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />
          <Pressable style={[styles.stackMenu, { top: menuPos.top, left: menuPos.left }]} onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            {menuOptions.map((opt) => (
              <TouchableOpacity key={opt.key} onPress={opt.onPress} activeOpacity={0.9} style={styles.stackBtn}>
                <Ionicons name={opt.icon} size={22} color={colors.textPrimary} style={styles.iconMargin} />
                <Text style={styles.stackLabel}>{opt.label}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </Pressable>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>

      <EditItemModal visible={editOpen} item={item} onClose={() => setEditOpen(false)} />
      <ShareModal visible={shareOpen} item={item} onClose={() => setShareOpen(false)} />
    </Swipeable>
  );
}
