import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState, useCallback, useRef } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator, Alert, Image, Modal, Pressable, ScrollView, Text, TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity, View, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, StyleSheet, Keyboard, InputAccessoryView, Button, Dimensions
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";
import { Video, ResizeMode, Audio } from 'expo-av';
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";

import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { getFileById, updateItem, setStarred, setImportant } from "../services/fileService";
import { emitDriveRefresh } from "../services/driveEvents";
import { downloadFile } from "../utils/downloadFile";

// ג… ׳™׳™׳‘׳•׳ Theme
import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";

const { width, height } = Dimensions.get("window");
const NEWLINE = "_@NL@_";
const ACCESSORY_ID = "unique_keyboard_accessory_id";

const isText = (m = "", n = "") =>
  m.startsWith("text/") || m === "application/json" || m === "application/javascript" || m === "application/xml" || /\.(txt|md|csv|log|xml|js)$/i.test(n || "");

const isImage = (m = "", n = "") =>
  m.startsWith("image/") || /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif|bmp|webp)$/i.test(n || "");

const isVideo = (m = "", n = "") =>
  m.startsWith("video/") || /\.(mp4|mov|mkv|avi|webm|m4v)$/i.test(n || "");

const isAudio = (m = "", n = "") =>
  m.startsWith("audio/") || /\.(mp3|wav|m4a|aac|flac|ogg)$/i.test(n || "");

function getIcon(m = "", n = "") {
  if (isImage(m, n)) return "image-outline";
  if (isVideo(m, n)) return "videocam-outline";
  if (isAudio(m, n)) return "musical-notes-outline";
  if (isText(m, n)) return "document-text-outline";
  if (m.includes("pdf") || /\.pdf$/i.test(n || "")) return "document-outline";
  if (m.includes("zip") || /\.(zip|rar|7z|tar|gz)$/i.test(n || "")) return "archive-outline";
  return "document-attach-outline";
}

function decodeJwtSafe(token) {
  try {
    if (!token) return {};
    const base64Url = token.split(".")[1];
    if (!base64Url) return {};
    const base64 = base64Url.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
    const padded = base64 + "===".slice((base64.length + 3) % 4);
    let jsonStr = "";
    if (typeof globalThis.atob === "function") {
      jsonStr = globalThis.atob(padded);
    } else {
      const { Buffer } = require("buffer");
      jsonStr = Buffer.from(padded, "base64").toString("utf8");
    }
    return JSON.parse(jsonStr);
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

function extractOwnerId(item) {
  return item?.ownerId || item?.owner?.id || item?.owner?._id || item?.owner || item?.userId || item?.createdBy || null;
}

export default function FilePreviewModal({ open, fileId, onClose }) {
  const { token } = useAuth();
  // ג… ׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳‘-Theme
  const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme();

  const [item, setItem] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  
  const [localMediaUri, setLocalMediaUri] = useState(null);
  const [mediaLoading, setMediaLoading] = useState(false);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);

  const [nameDraft, setNameDraft] = useState("");
  const [textDraft, setTextDraft] = useState("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const videoRef = useRef(null);

  const myUserId = useMemo(() => {
    const payload = decodeJwtSafe(token);
    return payload?.userId || payload?.id || payload?._id || null;
  }, [token]);

  const ownerId = useMemo(() => extractOwnerId(item), [item]);
  const effectiveRole = useMemo(() => {
    if (item?.role) return item.role;
    if (myUserId && ownerId && String(myUserId) === String(ownerId)) return "owner";
    return "viewer";
  }, [item, myUserId, ownerId]);

  const canEdit = effectiveRole === "owner" || effectiveRole === "editor";
  
  const mime = item?.mimeType || "";
  const fname = item?.name || "";
  
  const isTxt = useMemo(() => isText(mime, fname), [mime, fname]);
  const isImg = useMemo(() => isImage(mime, fname), [mime, fname]);
  const isVid = useMemo(() => isVideo(mime, fname), [mime, fname]);
  const isAud = useMemo(() => isAudio(mime, fname), [mime, fname]);

  const displayTxt = useMemo(() => String(item?.content || "").replaceAll(NEWLINE, "\n"), [item]);
  const safeTitle = useMemo(() => {
    const t = (nameDraft || item?.name || "Untitled").trim();
    return t.length ? t : "Untitled";
  }, [nameDraft, item]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const configureAudio = async () => {
      try {
        await Audio.setAudioModeAsync({
          allowsRecordingIOS: false,
          playsInSilentModeIOS: true,
          staysActiveInBackground: false,
          shouldDuckAndroid: true,
          playThroughEarpieceAndroid: false,
        });
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Failed to configure audio mode", e);
      }
    };
    if (open) configureAudio();
  }, [open]);

  const prepareMedia = useCallback(async (content, mimeType, fileName) => {
    if (!content) return;
    try {
        setMediaLoading(true);
        const tempName = (fileName || "temp").replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "_") + "_" + Date.now();
        const ext = mimeType.split("/")[1] || "mp4";
        const uri = FileSystem.cacheDirectory + `${tempName}.${ext}`;

        await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(uri, content, {
            encoding: 'base64'
        });

        setLocalMediaUri(uri);
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("Failed to prepare media:", e);
        Alert.alert("Error", "Could not load media for playback");
    } finally {
        setMediaLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!open || !token || !fileId) return;
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setLocalMediaUri(null);
      setIsPlaying(false);
      const data = await getFileById(token, fileId);
      setItem(data);
      setNameDraft(data?.name || "");
      setTextDraft(isText(data?.mimeType || "", data?.name || "") ? String(data?.content || "").replaceAll(NEWLINE, "\n") : "");

      if (isVideo(data.mimeType, data.name) || isAudio(data.mimeType, data.name)) {
          await prepareMedia(data.content, data.mimeType, data.name);
      }

    } catch (e) {
      Alert.alert("Error", e?.message || "Failed to open file");
      onClose?.(); 
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [open, token, fileId, prepareMedia]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) reload();
    if (!open) { 
        setItem(null); 
        setSaving(false); 
        setNameDraft(""); 
        setTextDraft(""); 
        setLocalMediaUri(null);
        setIsPlaying(false);
    }
  }, [open, reload]);

  const togglePlayback = () => {
      setIsPlaying(prev => !prev);
  };

  const onPlaybackStatusUpdate = (status) => {
      if (status.didJustFinish) {
          setIsPlaying(false);
          videoRef.current?.setPositionAsync(0);
      }
  };

  const hasNameChange = useMemo(() => !!item && safeTitle !== (item?.name || ""), [safeTitle, item]);
  const hasTextChange = useMemo(() => !!item && isTxt && (textDraft || "") !== (displayTxt || ""), [item, isTxt, textDraft, displayTxt]);
  const hasChanges = hasNameChange || hasTextChange;

  const onSave = async () => {
    if (!token || !item || !canEdit || !hasChanges) return;
    try {
      setSaving(true);
      const patch = {};
      if (hasNameChange) patch.name = safeTitle;
      if (isTxt && hasTextChange) {
        patch.content = String(textDraft || "").replaceAll("\n", NEWLINE);
        patch.mimeType = item.mimeType || "text/plain";
      }
      await updateItem(token, item.id, patch);
      emitDriveRefresh({ reason: "save", id: item.id });
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      setTimeout(() => { onClose?.(); }, 100);
    } catch (e) {
      Alert.alert("Save Failed", e?.message || "Could not save changes");
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDownload = async () => {
    try {
      if (!item?.content && !item?.url) return;
      const mime = item.mimeType || "";
      const isTextFile = mime.startsWith('text/') || mime.includes('json') || mime.includes('xml') || mime.includes('javascript') || mime.includes('html');

      await downloadFile({
        content: item.content,
        mimeType: mime,
        fileName: item.name || safeTitle,
        isBase64: !isTextFile 
      });
    } catch (e) {
      Alert.alert("Download failed", e?.message || "Could not download file");
    }
  };

  const toggleStar = async () => {
    if (!token || !item) return;
    const next = !item.starred;
    setItem((p) => ({ ...p, starred: next }));
    try {
      await setStarred(token, item.id, next);
      emitDriveRefresh({ reason: "star", id: item.id, value: next });
    } catch (e) { setItem((p) => ({ ...p, starred: !next })); }
  };

  const toggleImportant = async () => {
    if (!token || !item) return;
    const next = !item.important;
    setItem((p) => ({ ...p, important: next }));
    try {
      await setImportant(token, item.id, next);
      emitDriveRefresh({ reason: "important", id: item.id, value: next });
    } catch (e) { setItem((p) => ({ ...p, important: !next })); }
  };

  function IconBtn({ icon, onPress, disabled = false }) {
    return (
      <TouchableOpacity 
        onPress={onPress} 
        disabled={disabled} 
        activeOpacity={0.8} 
        style={[
            s.iconBtn, 
            disabled && { opacity: 0.45 },
            // ג… ׳¦׳‘׳¢ ׳›׳₪׳×׳•׳¨ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™
            { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.07)" }
        ]}
      >
        <Ionicons name={icon} size={20} color={colors.textPrimary} />
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Modal visible={open} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={onClose}>
      <View style={s.backdrop}>
        {/* ג… Blur ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™ */}
        <BlurView intensity={20} tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "light"} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />
        
        <Pressable style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} onPress={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); onClose?.(); }} />
        
        <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : "height"} style={s.centerWrap}>
          <Pressable 
            style={[
                s.card, 
                // ג… ׳¦׳‘׳¢ ׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™
                { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "#1e1e1e" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.94)" }
            ]} 
            onPress={() => {}}
          >
            
            {/* Header */}
            <View style={[s.header, { borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                {canEdit ? (
                  <View style={s.titleRow}>
                    <TextInput 
                        value={nameDraft} 
                        onChangeText={setNameDraft} 
                        style={[
                            s.titleInput, 
                            { 
                                color: colors.textPrimary, 
                                backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.05)"
                            }
                        ]} 
                        placeholder="Untitled" 
                        placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
                        returnKeyType="done" 
                        onSubmitEditing={Keyboard.dismiss} 
                    />
                    {hasNameChange && <View style={s.checkBadge}><Ionicons name="checkmark" size={16} color={colors.textPrimary} /></View>}
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  <Text style={[s.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{item?.name || ""}</Text>
                )}
                <Text style={[s.role, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Role: {effectiveRole}</Text>
              </View>
              <TouchableOpacity 
                style={[
                    s.closeBtn, 
                    { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.07)" }
                ]} 
                onPress={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); onClose?.(); }} 
                activeOpacity={0.85}
              >
                <Ionicons name="close" size={20} color={colors.textPrimary} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {/* Actions */}
            <View style={s.actionsRow}>
              <IconBtn icon={item?.starred ? "star" : "star-outline"} onPress={toggleStar} />
              <IconBtn icon={item?.important ? "alert" : "alert-circle-outline"} onPress={toggleImportant} />
              <IconBtn icon="download-outline" onPress={handleDownload} />
            </View>

            {/* Body */}
            <View style={s.body}>
              {loading ? (
                <View style={{ paddingVertical: 40, alignItems: "center" }}><ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} /></View>
              ) : !item ? null : isImg ? (
                <Image source={{ uri: `data:${item.mimeType};base64,${item.content}` }} style={s.image} />
              ) : isVid ? (
                <View style={s.mediaContainer}>
                    {mediaLoading ? (
                        <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" size="large" />
                    ) : localMediaUri ? (
                        <Video
                            ref={videoRef}
                            style={s.video}
                            source={{ uri: localMediaUri }}
                            useNativeControls={true}
                            resizeMode={ResizeMode.CONTAIN}
                            isLooping={false}
                            shouldPlay={true}
                            volume={1.0}
                            isMuted={false}
                        />
                    ) : (
                        <Text style={{color: '#fff'}}>Error loading video</Text>
                    )}
                </View>
              ) : isAud ? (
                <View style={[
                    s.mediaContainer, s.audioContainer,
                    { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.04)" }
                ]}>
                    {mediaLoading ? (
                        <ActivityIndicator color={colors.textPrimary} size="large" />
                    ) : localMediaUri ? (
                        <>
                            <View style={s.audioIconWrap}>
                                <Ionicons name="musical-notes" size={60} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                            </View>
                            
                            <TouchableOpacity onPress={togglePlayback} style={s.playPauseBtn}>
                                <Ionicons 
                                    name={isPlaying ? "pause-circle" : "play-circle"} 
                                    size={80} 
                                    color="#0d6efd" 
                                />
                            </TouchableOpacity>

                            <Text style={[s.audioText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{isPlaying ? "Playing..." : "Paused"}</Text>

                            <Video
                                ref={videoRef}
                                source={{ uri: localMediaUri }}
                                style={{ width: 1, height: 1, opacity: 0 }} 
                                resizeMode={ResizeMode.CONTAIN}
                                shouldPlay={isPlaying} 
                                isLooping={false}
                                volume={1.0} 
                                isMuted={false} 
                                onPlaybackStatusUpdate={onPlaybackStatusUpdate}
                                onError={(e) => console.log("Video Error:", e)}
                            />
                        </>
                    ) : (
                        <Text style={{color: colors.textPrimary}}>Error loading audio</Text>
                    )}
                </View>
              ) : isTxt ? (
                <View style={[
                    s.textBox, 
                    { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.04)" }
                ]}>
                  {canEdit ? (
                    <>
                      <TextInput 
                        value={textDraft} 
                        onChangeText={setTextDraft} 
                        multiline 
                        textAlignVertical="top" 
                        style={[s.textInput, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} 
                        placeholder="Type here..." 
                        placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
                        scrollEnabled 
                        inputAccessoryViewID={ACCESSORY_ID} 
                      />
                      {Platform.OS === "ios" && (
                        <InputAccessoryView nativeID={ACCESSORY_ID}>
                          <View style={s.accessory}><Button onPress={() => Keyboard.dismiss()} title="Done" /></View>
                        </InputAccessoryView>
                      )}
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <ScrollView keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
                        <Text style={[s.textRead, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{displayTxt}</Text>
                    </ScrollView>
                  )}
                </View>
              ) : (
                <View style={s.noPreview}>
                  <View style={s.bigIconWrap}>
                    <Ionicons name={getIcon(item.mimeType || "", item.name || "")} size={58} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                  </View>
                  <Text style={[s.fileName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{item?.name || safeTitle}</Text>
                  <Text style={[s.noPreviewText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>No preview available</Text>
                  <TouchableOpacity 
                    onPress={handleDownload} 
                    style={[
                        s.downloadBtn, 
                        { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.07)" }
                    ]} 
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                  >
                    <Text style={[s.downloadBtnText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Download / Open</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              )}
            </View>

            {/* Footer */}
            <View style={[s.footer, { borderTopColor: colors.border }]}>
              <TouchableOpacity 
                onPress={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); onClose?.(); }} 
                style={[
                    s.backBtn, 
                    { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.10)" }
                ]} 
                activeOpacity={0.9}
              >
                <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={18} color={colors.textPrimary} />
                <Text style={[s.backText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Back</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              {canEdit ? (
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={onSave} disabled={!hasChanges || saving} style={[s.saveBtn, (!hasChanges || saving) && { opacity: 0.5 }]} activeOpacity={0.9}>
                  <Text style={s.saveText}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ) : <View style={{ width: 1 }} />}
            </View>

          </Pressable>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const s = StyleSheet.create({
  backdrop: { flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" },
  dim: { backgroundColor: "rgba(0,0,0,0.10)" },
  centerWrap: { flex: 1, justifyContent: "center", padding: 18 },

import React from "react";
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import { formatBytes } from "../../../shared_code/utils/formatBytes";

export default function StorageTree({ usedBytes = 0, quotaBytes = 15 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024 }) {
  const used = Math.max(0, Number(usedBytes) || 0);
  const quota = Math.max(1, Number(quotaBytes) || 1);
  
  // ׳—׳™׳©׳•׳‘ ׳”׳׳—׳•׳–׳™׳
  const percentage = Math.min(100, (used / quota) * 100);

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* ׳׳™׳›׳ ׳”׳¢׳¥ */}
      <View style={styles.treeContainer}>
        {/* ׳©׳›׳‘׳” 1: ׳”׳¢׳¥ ׳”׳׳₪׳•׳¨ (׳¨׳§׳¢) */}
        <Text style={styles.treeIconBase}>נ´</Text>

        {/* ׳©׳›׳‘׳” 2: ׳”׳¢׳¥ ׳”׳¦׳‘׳¢׳•׳ ׳™ (׳׳™׳׳•׳™) - ׳ ׳—׳×׳ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳”׳’׳•׳‘׳” */}
        <View style={[styles.fillWrapper, { height: `${percentage}%` }]}>
          <Text style={styles.treeIconFill}>נ´</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* ׳˜׳§׳¡׳˜ ׳׳׳˜׳” */}
      <Text style={styles.text}>
        {formatBytes(used)} of {formatBytes(quota)} used
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    padding: 10,
    marginTop: "auto", // ׳“׳•׳—׳£ ׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳›׳™׳‘ ׳׳×׳—׳×׳™׳× ׳׳ ׳”׳•׳ ׳‘׳×׳•׳ Flex

  },

    fontSize: 50,
    position: "absolute",
    bottom: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    textAlign: "center",
    opacity: 0.3, // ׳׳₪׳•׳¨/׳©׳§׳•׳£
    color: "#5f6368",
    filter: "grayscale(100%)", // ׳”׳¢׳¨׳”: ׳‘-RN ׳–׳” ׳׳ ׳×׳׳™׳“ ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“, ׳”׳©׳§׳™׳₪׳•׳× ׳׳¡׳₪׳™׳§׳”
  },
  fillWrapper: {

    bottom: 0,

    justifyContent: "flex-end", // ׳׳¦׳׳™׳“ ׳׳× ׳”׳˜׳§׳¡׳˜ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳׳™ ׳׳×׳—׳×׳™׳×

===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\components\UploadPreviewContent.jsx =====
import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  View, Text, TextInput, Image, TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView, ActivityIndicator, Alert, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { Video, ResizeMode } from "expo-av";

import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";
import { getUploadPreviewStyles } from "../styles/uploadPreview.styles";

// Helper functions for file type detection
const isImage = (mimeType = "") => mimeType.startsWith("image/");
const isVideo = (mimeType = "") => mimeType.startsWith("video/");

export default function UploadPreviewContent({
  fileData,        // { uri, fileName, mimeType }
  folders,         // Array of available folders for destination selection
  onConfirm,       // (finalFileName, destinationFolderId) => void
  onDiscard,       // () => void - go back/cancel
  onChangeFile,    // () => void - pick a different file
  uploading,       // boolean - show loading state during upload
}) {
  const { colors = {} } = useTheme();
  const styles = useMemo(() => getUploadPreviewStyles(colors), [colors]);
  
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(fileData?.fileName || "");
  const [selectedFolderId, setSelectedFolderId] = useState(null); // null = root
  const [showFolderPicker, setShowFolderPicker] = useState(false);

  // Reset title when fileData changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (fileData?.fileName) {
      setTitle(fileData.fileName);
    }
  }, [fileData?.fileName]);

  const selectedFolderName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedFolderId) return "My Drive (Root)";
    const folder = folders?.find(f => f.id === selectedFolderId);
    return folder?.name || "Unknown Folder";
  }, [selectedFolderId, folders]);

  const handleConfirm = () => {
    if (!title.trim()) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Please enter a file name");
      return;
    }
    onConfirm?.(title.trim(), selectedFolderId);
  };

  if (!fileData) return null;

  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView 
      behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : "height"} 
      style={styles.centerWrap}
    >
      <View style={styles.card}>
        {/* Header */}
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Review Upload</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={onDiscard} style={styles.closeBtn}>
            <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color={colors.textPrimary || "#333"} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <ScrollView style={styles.body} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
          {/* Preview */}
          <View style={styles.previewContainer}>
            {isImage(fileData.mimeType) ? (
              <Image source={{ uri: fileData.uri }} style={styles.previewImage} resizeMode="contain" />
            ) : isVideo(fileData.mimeType) ? (
              <Video
                source={{ uri: fileData.uri }}
                style={styles.previewVideo}
                useNativeControls
                resizeMode={ResizeMode.CONTAIN}
              />
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.previewFallback}>
                <Ionicons name="document-outline" size={60} color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} />
                <Text style={styles.previewFallbackText}>
                  {fileData.mimeType || "File"}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          {/* Title Input */}
          <Text style={styles.label}>File Name</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={title}
            onChangeText={setTitle}
            placeholder="Enter file name..."
            placeholderTextColor={colors.textSecondary || "#999"}
          />

          {/* Destination Picker */}
          <Text style={styles.label}>Save to</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity 
            style={styles.folderPicker} 
            onPress={() => setShowFolderPicker(!showFolderPicker)}
          >
            <Ionicons name="folder-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} />
            <Text style={styles.folderPickerText}>{selectedFolderName}</Text>
            <Ionicons 
              name={showFolderPicker ? "chevron-up" : "chevron-down"} 
              size={18} 
              color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} 
            />
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {/* Folder Selection List (expandable) */}
          {showFolderPicker && (
            <View style={styles.folderList}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.folderItem, 
                  !selectedFolderId && styles.folderItemActive
                ]}
                onPress={() => { 
                  setSelectedFolderId(null); 
                  setShowFolderPicker(false); 
                }}
              >
                <Ionicons name="home-outline" size={18} color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} />
                <Text style={styles.folderItemText}>My Drive (Root)</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              
              {folders?.filter(f => f.type === "folder").map(folder => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={folder.id}
                  style={[
                    styles.folderItem, 
                    selectedFolderId === folder.id && styles.folderItemActive
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => { 
                    setSelectedFolderId(folder.id); 
                    setShowFolderPicker(false); 
                  }}
                >
                  <Ionicons name="folder" size={18} color="#f59e0b" />
                  <Text style={styles.folderItemText}>{folder.name}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </View>
          )}
        </ScrollView>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <View style={styles.footer}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.secondaryBtn} onPress={onChangeFile}>
            <Ionicons name="refresh-outline" size={18} color={colors.textPrimary || "#333"} />
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Change</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.discardBtn} onPress={onDiscard}>
            <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={18} color="#dc2626" />
            <Text style={styles.discardBtnText}>Discard</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity 
            style={[
              styles.confirmBtn, 
              uploading && styles.confirmBtnDisabled
            ]} 
            onPress={handleConfirm}
            disabled={uploading}
          >
            {uploading ? (
              <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" size="small" />
            ) : (
              <>
                <Ionicons name="cloud-upload-outline" size={18} color="#fff" />
                <Text style={styles.confirmBtnText}>Upload</Text>
              </>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\hooks\useDriveUploads.js =====
import { useState, useRef } from "react";
import { Alert } from "react-native";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import * as DocumentPicker from "expo-document-picker";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import { createItem } from "../services/fileService";
import { emitDriveRefresh } from "../services/driveEvents";

export function useDriveUploads(token, onRefresh, closeCallback) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [pendingFile, setPendingFile] = useState(null); // { uri, fileName, mimeType }
  const pickerInProgressRef = useRef(false);

  const lockPicker = () => {
    if (pickerInProgressRef.current) return false;
    pickerInProgressRef.current = true;
    return true;
  };

  const unlockPicker = () => {
    pickerInProgressRef.current = false;
  };

  // Called after user confirms from preview screen
  const confirmUpload = async (fileName, parentId = null) => {
    if (!pendingFile || !token) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "No file selected or not logged in.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      // ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳”׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ ׳›-Base64 (׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳׳§׳‘׳¦׳™׳ ׳‘׳’׳•׳“׳ ׳¡׳‘׳™׳¨)
      const base64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(pendingFile.uri, { encoding: "base64" });

      await createItem(token, {
        name: fileName,
        type: "file",
        content: base64,
        mimeType: pendingFile.mimeType || "application/octet-stream",
        parentId, // destination folder
      });

      emitDriveRefresh({ reason: "upload", name: fileName });
      onRefresh?.();
      setPendingFile(null);
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Upload Failed", err?.message || "Unknown error");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      unlockPicker();
      closeCallback?.();
    }
  };

  // Discard the pending file without uploading
  const discardPending = () => {
    setPendingFile(null);
    unlockPicker();
    closeCallback?.();
  };

  const pickDocument = async () => {
    if (!lockPicker()) return;
    try {
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));
      
      const result = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({
        copyToCacheDirectory: true,
        type: "*/*", // ׳׳׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳¡׳•׳’׳™ ׳”׳§׳‘׳¦׳™׳
        multiple: false,
      });
      if (result?.canceled) {
        unlockPicker();
        return;
      }

      const asset = result?.assets?.[0];
      if (asset?.uri) {
        const fileName = asset.name || `file_${Date.now()}`;
        // Set pending file - same modal will show preview content
        unlockPicker();
        setPendingFile({
          uri: asset.uri,
          fileName,
          mimeType: asset.mimeType,
        });
      } else {
        unlockPicker();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Picker failed");
      unlockPicker();
    }
  };

  const pickImage = async (useCamera) => {
    if (!lockPicker()) return;
    try {
      const permission = useCamera
        ? await ImagePicker.requestCameraPermissionsAsync()
        : await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();

      if (!permission.granted) {
        Alert.alert("Permission Denied", "Settings required.");
        unlockPicker();
        return;
      }

      const options = {
        // ג… ׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™ ׳§׳¨׳™׳˜׳™: ׳׳׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳’׳ ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳•׳’׳ ׳•׳™׳“׳׳•
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.All, 
        quality: 0.7,
        allowsEditing: false, // ׳¢׳¨׳™׳›׳” ׳‘׳•׳™׳“׳׳• ׳׳₪׳¢׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳¢׳™׳™׳×׳™׳× ׳‘׳׳ ׳“׳¨׳•׳׳™׳“, ׳¢׳“׳™׳£ ׳׳›׳‘׳•׳×
      };

      const result = useCamera
        ? await ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync(options)
        : await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync(options);
      if (result?.canceled) {
        unlockPicker();
        return;
      }

      const asset = result?.assets?.[0];
      if (asset?.uri) {
        let fileName = asset.fileName;
        
        if (!fileName) {
          const uriParts = String(asset.uri).split("/");
          const defaultExt = asset.type === 'video' ? '.mp4' : '.jpg';
          fileName = uriParts[uriParts.length - 1] || `media_${Date.now()}${defaultExt}`;
        }
        
        // ׳•׳™׳“׳•׳ ׳¡׳™׳•׳׳×
        if (fileName && !fileName.includes(".")) {
            fileName += asset.type === 'video' ? ".mp4" : ".jpg";
        }
        
        // ׳§׳‘׳™׳¢׳× MimeType ׳‘׳¨׳™׳¨׳× ׳׳—׳“׳ ׳׳ ׳׳™׳
        const mime = asset.mimeType || (asset.type === 'video' ? "video/mp4" : "image/jpeg");

        // Set pending file - same modal will show preview content
        unlockPicker();
        setPendingFile({
          uri: asset.uri,
          fileName,
          mimeType: mime,
        });
      } else {
        unlockPicker();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Action failed");
      unlockPicker();
    }
  };

  return { 
    loading, 
    pickDocument, 
    pickImage,
    pendingFile,      // The file awaiting review
    confirmUpload,    // Call to confirm and upload
    discardPending,   // Call to discard without uploading
  };
}
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\hooks\useMyDrive.js =====
// mobile-client/src/features/drive/hooks/useMyDrive.js

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { getFiles } from "../services/fileService";
import { subscribeDriveRefresh } from "../services/driveEvents";

export function useMyDrive() {
  const { token, logout } = useAuth();

  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);

  const fetchItems = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!token) return;

    try {
      const data = await getFiles(token);
      setItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error?.message &&
        (error.message.includes("token") || error.message.includes("401"))
      ) {
        await logout();
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [token, logout]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchItems();
  }, [fetchItems]);

  // ג… listen for global refresh events (upload/create/delete/etc.)
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeDriveRefresh(() => {
      setRefreshing(true);
      fetchItems();
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, [fetchItems]);

  const onRefresh = () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    fetchItems();
  };

  return { items, loading, refreshing, onRefresh };
}
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\modals\EditItemModal.jsx =====
import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  View, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, Modal,
  ScrollView, ActivityIndicator, Alert, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform,
  Pressable, StyleSheet
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";

import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";
import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { getFileIconMeta } from "../utils/fileIcon";
import { updateItem, getFiles } from "../services/fileService";
import { emitDriveRefresh } from "../services/driveEvents";
import { getEditItemModalStyles } from "../styles/editItemModal.styles";

export default function EditItemModal({ visible, item, onClose }) {
  const { colors = {}, isDarkMode } = useTheme();
  const { token } = useAuth();
  const styles = useMemo(() => getEditItemModalStyles(colors), [colors]);

  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [selectedFolderId, setSelectedFolderId] = useState(null);
  const [showFolderPicker, setShowFolderPicker] = useState(false);
  const [folders, setFolders] = useState([]);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const isFolder = item?.type === "folder";
  const { icon, color } = getFileIconMeta(item?.name, item?.mimeType, isFolder);

  // Reset form when item changes or modal opens
  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible && item) {
      setName(item.name || "");
      setSelectedFolderId(item.parentId || null);
      setShowFolderPicker(false);
      fetchFolders();
    }
  }, [visible, item?.id]);

  const fetchFolders = async () => {
    if (!token) return;
    try {
      const files = await getFiles(token);
      // Filter out the current item if it's a folder (can't move folder into itself)
      const foldersList = (files || [])
        .filter(f => f.type === "folder" && f.id !== item?.id);
      setFolders(foldersList);
    } catch (err) {
      // Silently fail
    }
  };

  const selectedFolderName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedFolderId) return "My Drive (Root)";
    const folder = folders?.find(f => f.id === selectedFolderId);
    return folder?.name || "Unknown Folder";
  }, [selectedFolderId, folders]);

  const handleSave = async () => {
    const trimmedName = name.trim();
    if (!trimmedName) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Please enter a name");
      return;
    }
    if (!token || !item?.id) return;

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await updateItem(token, item.id, {
        name: trimmedName,
        parentId: selectedFolderId,
      });
      emitDriveRefresh({ reason: "edit", name: trimmedName });
      onClose?.();
    } catch (e) {
      Alert.alert("Error", e?.message || "Failed to save changes");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  if (!visible) return null;

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade">
      <Pressable style={styles.backdrop} onPress={onClose}>
        <BlurView intensity={30} tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "light"} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />
        
        <KeyboardAvoidingView 
          behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : "height"} 
          style={styles.centerWrap}
        >
          <Pressable 
            style={styles.card}
            onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            {/* Header */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
              <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>
                Edit {isFolder ? "Folder" : "File"}
              </Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeBtn}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color={colors.textPrimary || "#333"} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <ScrollView style={styles.body} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
              {/* File/Folder Icon Preview */}
              <View style={styles.previewContainer}>
                <View style={styles.iconPreview}>
                  <Ionicons name={icon} size={60} color={color} />
                </View>
              </View>

              {/* Name Input */}
              <Text style={styles.label}>Name</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.input}
                value={name}
                onChangeText={setName}
                placeholder={isFolder ? "Folder name..." : "File name..."}
                placeholderTextColor={colors.textSecondary || "#999"}
                autoFocus
              />

              {/* Folder Picker */}
              <Text style={styles.label}>Location</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity 
                style={styles.folderPicker}
                onPress={() => setShowFolderPicker(!showFolderPicker)}
              >
                <Ionicons name="folder-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} />
                <Text style={styles.folderPickerText}>
                  {selectedFolderName}
                </Text>
                <Ionicons 
                  name={showFolderPicker ? "chevron-up" : "chevron-down"} 
                  size={18} 
                  color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} 
                />
              </TouchableOpacity>

              {/* Folder Selection List */}
              {showFolderPicker && (
                <View style={styles.folderList}>
                  <ScrollView nestedScrollEnabled keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={[
                        styles.folderItem, 
                        !selectedFolderId && styles.folderItemActive
                      ]}
                      onPress={() => { 
                        setSelectedFolderId(null); 
                        setShowFolderPicker(false); 
                      }}
                    >
                      <Ionicons name="home-outline" size={18} color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} />
                      <Text style={styles.folderItemText}>
                        My Drive (Root)
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    
                    {folders?.map(folder => (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={folder.id}
                        style={[
                          styles.folderItem, 
                          selectedFolderId === folder.id && styles.folderItemActive
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => { 
                          setSelectedFolderId(folder.id); 
                          setShowFolderPicker(false); 
                        }}
                      >
                        <Ionicons name="folder" size={18} color="#f59e0b" />
                        <Text style={styles.folderItemText}>
                          {folder.name}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    ))}
                  </ScrollView>
                </View>
              )}
            </ScrollView>

            {/* Footer Actions */}
            <View style={styles.footer}>
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.cancelBtn} onPress={onClose}>
                <Text style={styles.cancelBtnText}>CANCEL</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity 
                style={[styles.saveBtn, saving && styles.saveBtnDisabled]} 
                onPress={handleSave}
                disabled={saving}
              >
                {saving ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" size="small" />
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Ionicons name="checkmark" size={18} color="#fff" />
                    <Text style={styles.saveBtnText}>SAVE</Text>
                  </>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </Pressable>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </Pressable>
    </Modal>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Modal, View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, Image, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';

import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useAuth } from '../../../shared_code/hooks/useAuth';

export default function EditUserModal({ visible, onClose }) {

    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        firstName: user?.firstName || '',

        phoneNumber: user?.phoneNumber || '',

            allowsEditing: true, aspect: [1, 1], quality: 0.7,

        await updateUserData(data);
        onClose();
    };

    return (
        <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade">
            <View style={styles.overlay}>
                <View style={styles.container}>

                    <View style={styles.imageSection}>

                    <TextInput
                        style={styles.input}
                        value={formData.firstName}
                        onChangeText={(t) => setFormData({ ...formData, firstName: t })}
                        placeholder="FIRST NAME"

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import {
    Modal, View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, FlatList,
    ActivityIndicator, Alert, StyleSheet, TouchableOpacity, Image, Dimensions, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";
import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import {
    getPermissions, addPermission, updatePermission, deletePermission, findUserByEmail,
} from "../services/permissionService";
import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";
import { getDriveStyles } from "../styles/myDrive.styles";

const { width: SCREEN_WIDTH } = Dimensions.get("window");

export default function ShareModal({ visible, item, onClose }) {
    const { token, user } = useAuth();
    const [permissions, setPermissions] = useState([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
    const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme();

    const fileId = item?.id || item?._id;
    const isOwner = String(item?.ownerId || item?.owner) === String(user?.id || user?._id);
    const driveStyles = useMemo(() => getDriveStyles(colors), [colors]);

    const loadPermissions = useCallback(async () => {
        if (!fileId || !token) return;
        setLoading(true);
        try {
            const data = await getPermissions(token, fileId);
            setPermissions(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        } catch (e) {
            Alert.alert("Error", "Failed to load permissions");
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    }, [fileId, token]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (visible && fileId) loadPermissions();
    }, [visible, fileId, loadPermissions]);

    const handleShare = async () => {
        if (!email.trim()) return;
        setAdding(true);
        try {
            const foundUser = await findUserByEmail(token, email.trim());
            if (!foundUser?.id && !foundUser?._id) {
                Alert.alert("User Not Found", "No user with that email exists.");
                return;
            }
            await addPermission(token, fileId, foundUser.id || foundUser._id, "viewer");
            setEmail("");
            loadPermissions();
        } catch (e) {
            Alert.alert("Error", e?.message || "Failed to share");
        } finally {
            setAdding(false);
        }
    };

    const handleToggleRole = async (perm) => {
        const newRole = perm.role === "viewer" ? "editor" : "viewer";
        try {
            const permId = perm.id || perm._id;
            await updatePermission(token, fileId, permId, newRole);
            await loadPermissions();
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("Update role error:", e);
            Alert.alert("Error", "Failed to update role");
        }
    };

    const handleRemove = async (perm) => {
        Alert.alert(
            "Remove Access",
            `Are you sure you want to remove access for ${perm.email}?`,
            [
                { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
                {
                    text: "Remove",
                    style: "destructive",
                    onPress: async () => {
                        try {
                            const permId = perm.id || perm._id;
                            await deletePermission(token, fileId, permId);
                            await loadPermissions(); // ׳¨׳¢׳ ׳•׳ ׳”׳¨׳©׳™׳׳”
                        } catch (e) {
                            console.error("Delete permission error:", e);
                            Alert.alert("Error", "Failed to remove user");
                        }
                    },
                },
            ]
        );
    };

    if (!visible) return null;

    return (
        <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={onClose}>
            <View style={styles.overlay}>
                {/* ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳׳˜׳•׳©׳˜׳© ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳׳™׳׳”/׳™׳•׳ */}
                <BlurView intensity={20} tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "dark"} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />
                <Pressable style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} onPress={onClose} />

                <KeyboardAvoidingView
                    behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : "height"}
                    style={styles.container}
                >
                    {/* ׳›׳¨׳˜׳™׳¡׳™׳™׳× ׳”׳–׳›׳•׳›׳™׳× - ׳¦׳‘׳¢ ׳”׳¨׳§׳¢ (card) ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳” ׳׳₪׳™ ׳”-Theme */}
                    <BlurView intensity={95} tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "light"} style={[styles.glassCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card + 'F2' }]}>
                        <View style={styles.header}>
                            <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary, fontFamily: 'BrunoAceSC_400Regular' }]}>
                                SHARE FILE
                            </Text>
                            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeIcon}>
                                <Ionicons name="close" size={24} color={colors.textPrimary} />
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>

                        {isOwner && (
                            <View style={styles.addSection}>
                                <View style={[styles.inputWrapper, { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                                    <TextInput
                                        style={[styles.input, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}
                                        placeholder="Enter email..."
                                        placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
                                        value={email}
                                        onChangeText={setEmail}
                                        keyboardType="email-address"
                                        autoCapitalize="none"
                                    />
                                    <TouchableOpacity
                                        style={[styles.addBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}
                                        onPress={handleShare}
                                        disabled={adding}
                                    >
                                        {adding ? (
                                            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#fff" />
                                        ) : (
                                            <Ionicons name="person-add" size={20} color="#fff" />
                                        )}
                                    </TouchableOpacity>
                                </View>
                            </View>
                        )}

                        <View style={styles.listContainer}>
                            <Text style={[styles.sectionLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>PEOPLE WITH ACCESS</Text>
                            {loading ? (
                                <ActivityIndicator style={{ marginVertical: 20 }} color={colors.primary} />
                            ) : (
                                <FlatList
                                    data={permissions}
                                    keyExtractor={(p) => p.id || p.userId || Math.random().toString()}
                                    renderItem={({ item: perm }) => (
                                        <View style={[styles.permItem, { borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
                                            <View style={styles.avatarWrapper}>
                                                {perm.image ? (
                                                    <Image source={{ uri: perm.image }} style={styles.avatar} />
                                                ) : (
                                                    <View style={[styles.avatar, styles.avatarPlaceholder, { backgroundColor: colors.primary + '26' }]}>
                                                        <Ionicons name="person" size={18} color={colors.primary} />
                                                    </View>
                                                )}
                                            </View>

                                            <View style={styles.userInfo}>
                                                <Text style={[styles.userName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                                    {perm.firstName} {perm.lastName}
                                                </Text>
                                                <Text style={[styles.userEmail, { color: colors.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                                    {perm.email}
                                                </Text>
                                            </View>

                                            <View style={styles.actionsContainer}>
                                                {isOwner ? (
                                                    <>
                                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                                            style={[styles.roleBadgeActive, { backgroundColor: colors.primary + '26', borderColor: colors.primary + '33' }]}
                                                            onPress={() => handleToggleRole(perm)}
                                                        >
                                                            <Text style={[styles.roleTextActive, { color: colors.primary }]}>{perm.role?.toUpperCase()}</Text>
                                                            <Ionicons name="chevron-down" size={12} color={colors.primary} style={{ marginLeft: 4 }} />
                                                        </TouchableOpacity>

                                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                                            style={[styles.deleteBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.danger + '1A' }]}
                                                            onPress={() => handleRemove(perm)}
                                                        >
                                                            <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={18} color={colors.danger} />
                                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                                    </>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <View style={[styles.roleBadgeReadOnly, { backgroundColor: colors.surface }]}>
                                                        <Text style={[styles.roleTextReadOnly, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{perm.role?.toUpperCase()}</Text>
                                                    </View>
                                                )}
                                            </View>
                                        </View>
                                    )}
                                    ListEmptyComponent={
                                        <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: colors.textTertiary }]}>No shared users found.</Text>
                                    }
                                />
                            )}
                        </View>
                    </BlurView>
                </KeyboardAvoidingView>
            </View>
        </Modal>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  Modal, View, Text, TextInput, Image, TouchableOpacity,
  StyleSheet, Pressable, ScrollView, ActivityIndicator, 
  Alert, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";
import { Video, ResizeMode } from "expo-av";

import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";
import { getUploadPreviewStyles } from "../styles/uploadPreview.styles";

// Helper functions for file type detection
const isImage = (mimeType = "") => mimeType.startsWith("image/");
const isVideo = (mimeType = "") => mimeType.startsWith("video/");

export default function UploadPreviewModal({
  visible,
  fileData,        // { uri, fileName, mimeType }
  folders,         // Array of available folders for destination selection
  onConfirm,       // (finalFileName, destinationFolderId) => void
  onDiscard,       // () => void - go back/cancel
  onChangeFile,    // () => void - pick a different file
  uploading,       // boolean - show loading state during upload
}) {
  const { colors = {}, isDarkMode } = useTheme();
  const styles = useMemo(() => getUploadPreviewStyles(colors), [colors]);
  
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(fileData?.fileName || "");
  const [selectedFolderId, setSelectedFolderId] = useState(null); // null = root
  const [showFolderPicker, setShowFolderPicker] = useState(false);

  // Reset title when fileData changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (fileData?.fileName) {
      setTitle(fileData.fileName);
    }
  }, [fileData?.fileName]);

  const selectedFolderName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedFolderId) return "My Drive (Root)";
    const folder = folders?.find(f => f.id === selectedFolderId);
    return folder?.name || "Unknown Folder";
  }, [selectedFolderId, folders]);

  const handleConfirm = () => {
    if (!title.trim()) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Please enter a file name");
      return;
    }
    onConfirm?.(title.trim(), selectedFolderId);
  };

  const isVisible = visible && !!fileData;

  return (
    <Modal
      visible={isVisible}
      transparent
      animationType="fade"
      presentationStyle="overFullScreen"
      onRequestClose={onDiscard}
    >
      {fileData && (
        <View style={styles.backdrop}>
          <BlurView 
            intensity={20} 
            tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "light"} 
            style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} 
          />
          <Pressable style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} onPress={onDiscard} />
          
          <KeyboardAvoidingView 
            behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : "height"} 
            style={styles.centerWrap}
          >
            <Pressable style={styles.card} onPress={() => {}}>
              {/* Header */}
              <View style={styles.header}>
                <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Review Upload</Text>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={onDiscard} style={styles.closeBtn}>
                  <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color={colors.textPrimary || "#333"} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              <ScrollView style={styles.body} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                {/* Preview */}
                <View style={styles.previewContainer}>
                  {isImage(fileData.mimeType) ? (
                    <Image source={{ uri: fileData.uri }} style={styles.previewImage} resizeMode="contain" />
                  ) : isVideo(fileData.mimeType) ? (
                    <Video
                      source={{ uri: fileData.uri }}
                      style={styles.previewVideo}
                      useNativeControls
                      resizeMode={ResizeMode.CONTAIN}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <View style={styles.previewFallback}>
                      <Ionicons name="document-outline" size={60} color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} />
                      <Text style={styles.previewFallbackText}>
                        {fileData.mimeType || "File"}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>

                {/* Title Input */}
                <Text style={styles.label}>File Name</Text>
                <TextInput
                  style={styles.input}
                  value={title}
                  onChangeText={setTitle}
                  placeholder="Enter file name..."
                  placeholderTextColor={colors.textSecondary || "#999"}
                />

                {/* Destination Picker */}
                <Text style={styles.label}>Save to</Text>
                <TouchableOpacity 
                  style={styles.folderPicker} 
                  onPress={() => setShowFolderPicker(!showFolderPicker)}
                >
                  <Ionicons name="folder-outline" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} />
                  <Text style={styles.folderPickerText}>{selectedFolderName}</Text>
                  <Ionicons 
                    name={showFolderPicker ? "chevron-up" : "chevron-down"} 
                    size={18} 
                    color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} 
                  />
                </TouchableOpacity>

                {/* Folder Selection List (expandable) */}
                {showFolderPicker && (
                  <View style={styles.folderList}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={[
                        styles.folderItem, 
                        !selectedFolderId && styles.folderItemActive
                      ]}
                      onPress={() => { 
                        setSelectedFolderId(null); 
                        setShowFolderPicker(false); 
                      }}
                    >
                      <Ionicons name="home-outline" size={18} color={colors.textSecondary || "#666"} />
                      <Text style={styles.folderItemText}>My Drive (Root)</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    
                    {folders?.filter(f => f.type === "folder").map(folder => (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={folder.id}
                        style={[
                          styles.folderItem, 
                          selectedFolderId === folder.id && styles.folderItemActive
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => { 
                          setSelectedFolderId(folder.id); 
                          setShowFolderPicker(false); 
                        }}
                      >
                        <Ionicons name="folder" size={18} color="#f59e0b" />
                        <Text style={styles.folderItemText}>{folder.name}</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    ))}
                  </View>
                )}
              </ScrollView>

              {/* Footer Actions */}
              <View style={styles.footer}>
                <TouchableOpacity style={styles.secondaryBtn} onPress={onChangeFile}>
                  <Ionicons name="refresh-outline" size={18} color={colors.textPrimary || "#333"} />
                  <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Change</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity style={styles.discardBtn} onPress={onDiscard}>
                  <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={18} color="#dc2626" />
                  <Text style={styles.discardBtnText}>Discard</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity 
                  style={[
                    styles.confirmBtn, 
                    uploading && styles.confirmBtnDisabled
                  ]} 
                  onPress={handleConfirm}
                  disabled={uploading}
                >
                  {uploading ? (
                    <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" size="small" />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Ionicons name="cloud-upload-outline" size={18} color="#fff" />
                      <Text style={styles.confirmBtnText}>Upload</Text>
                    </>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </Pressable>
          </KeyboardAvoidingView>
        </View>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}

import React, { useMemo, useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { View, Text, FlatList, ActivityIndicator, Pressable, Alert, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

import { useMyDrive } from "../hooks/useMyDrive";
import DriveListItem from "../components/DriveListItem";
import FilePreviewModal from "../components/FilePreviewModal";
import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { moveToTrash } from "../services/fileService";
import { emitDriveRefresh } from "../services/driveEvents";

// ג… ׳™׳™׳‘׳•׳ Theme
import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";

export default function MainHomePage() {
  const [currentFolderId, setCurrentFolderId] = useState(null); 
  const [folderPath, setFolderPath] = useState([]);

  const { items, loading, refreshing, onRefresh } = useMyDrive();
  const { token } = useAuth();
  
  // ג… ׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳‘׳¦׳‘׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳”-Theme
  const { colors } = useTheme();

  const currentItems = useMemo(() => {
      if (!items) return [];
      return items.filter(item => {
          if (currentFolderId === null) return !item.parentId; 
          return item.parentId === currentFolderId;
      });
  }, [items, currentFolderId]);

  const folders = useMemo(() => currentItems.filter((i) => i?.type === "folder"), [currentItems]);
  const files = useMemo(() => currentItems.filter((i) => i?.type === "file"), [currentItems]);

  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null);

  const openItem = useCallback((item) => {
    if (!item) return;
    if (item.type === "folder") {
      setCurrentFolderId(item.id);
      setFolderPath((prev) => [...prev, { id: item.id, name: item.name }]);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedId(item.id);
    setPreviewOpen(true);
  }, []);

  const handleDeleteItem = useCallback(async (item) => {
    Alert.alert("Move to Trash?", `Delete "${item.name}"?`, [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      {
        text: "Delete", style: "destructive",
        onPress: async () => {
          try { await moveToTrash(token, item.id); emitDriveRefresh(); } 
          catch (err) { Alert.alert("Error", "Could not delete item."); }
        }
      }
    ]);
  }, [token]);

  const handleBack = useCallback(() => {
    if (folderPath.length === 0) return;
    const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, -1);
    setFolderPath(newPath);
    setCurrentFolderId(newPath.length > 0 ? newPath[newPath.length - 1].id : null);
  }, [folderPath]);

  const navigateToBreadcrumb = useCallback((index) => {
    if (index < 0) {
      setFolderPath([]);
      setCurrentFolderId(null);
    } else {
      const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, index + 1);
      setFolderPath(newPath);
      setCurrentFolderId(newPath[newPath.length - 1].id);
    }
  }, [folderPath]);

  // Loader state
  if (loading && (!items || items.length === 0)) {
    return (
       <View style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' }}>
           <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
       </View>
    );
  }

  // ׳›׳•׳×׳¨׳× ׳₪׳ ׳™׳׳™׳× ׳§׳˜׳ ׳” (Suggested folders / files)
  const SectionHeader = ({ icon, title }) => (
    <View style={styles.sectionTitleRow}>
        <Ionicons name={icon} size={18} color={colors.textPrimary} />
        <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{title}</Text>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}> 
      {/* ׳”׳¡׳¨׳ ׳• ׳׳× ׳”-ScreenLayout ׳•׳׳× ׳”׳›׳•׳×׳¨׳× "HOME" ׳׳›׳׳ 
         ׳›׳™ ׳”׳™׳ ׳›׳‘׳¨ ׳׳•׳₪׳™׳¢׳” ׳‘-HomeManager ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳™ 
      */}

      <FlatList
        data={[]}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 15, paddingTop: 10 }}>
            
            {/* ג… ׳₪׳™׳¨׳•׳¨׳™ ׳׳—׳ (Breadcrumbs) - ׳׳•׳₪׳™׳¢׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳›׳©׳ ׳›׳ ׳¡׳™׳ ׳׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳” */}
            {folderPath.length > 0 && (
                <View style={styles.breadcrumbContainer}>
                    <Pressable onPress={handleBack} style={[styles.backButton, { backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={18} color={colors.textPrimary} />
                    </Pressable>
                    <Pressable onPress={() => navigateToBreadcrumb(-1)}>
                        <Text style={[styles.breadcrumbText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Home</Text>
                    </Pressable>
                    {folderPath.map((folder, index) => (
                        <View key={folder.id} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={14} color={colors.textTertiary} />
                            <Pressable onPress={() => navigateToBreadcrumb(index)}>
                                <Text style={[
                                    styles.breadcrumbText,
                                    { 
                                        color: index === folderPath.length - 1 ? colors.primary : colors.textSecondary, 
                                        fontWeight: index === folderPath.length - 1 ? '700' : '400' 
                                    }
                                ]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                    {folder.name}
                                </Text>
                            </Pressable>
                        </View>
                    ))}
                </View>
            )}

            {/* ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳× ׳”׳×׳™׳§׳™׳•׳× */}
            {folders.length > 0 && (
                <View style={styles.sectionWrapper}>
                    <SectionHeader icon="folder-open-outline" title="Suggested folders" />
                    {folders.map((item) => (
                        <DriveListItem key={item.id} item={item} onPress={openItem} onDelete={handleDeleteItem} />
                    ))}
                </View>
            )}

            {/* ׳¨׳©׳™׳׳× ׳”׳§׳‘׳¦׳™׳ */}
            {files.length > 0 && (
                <View style={styles.sectionWrapper}>
                    <SectionHeader icon="document-text-outline" title="Suggested files" />
                    {files.map((item) => (
                        <DriveListItem key={item.id} item={item} onPress={openItem} onDelete={handleDeleteItem} />
                    ))}
                </View>
            )}

            {/* ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳¨׳™׳§ */}
            {folders.length === 0 && files.length === 0 && (
                <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 80, opacity: 0.7 }}>
                    <Ionicons name="cloud-offline-outline" size={60} color={colors.textTertiary} />
                    <Text style={{ marginTop: 10, color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 16 }}>
                        Empty Folder
                    </Text>
                </View>
            )}
          </View>
        }
        refreshing={refreshing}
        onRefresh={onRefresh}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 100 }} // ׳׳¨׳•׳•׳— ׳׳׳˜׳” ׳‘׳©׳‘׳™׳ ׳”-Fab ׳•׳”-BottomBar
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      />

      <FilePreviewModal
        open={previewOpen}
        fileId={selectedId}
        onClose={() => setPreviewOpen(false)}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    breadcrumbContainer: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        flexWrap: 'wrap',
        marginBottom: 15,
        gap: 5
    },
    backButton: {
        padding: 6,
        borderRadius: 8,
        marginRight: 5
    },

        fontSize: 15,

        marginBottom: 20

        letterSpacing: 0.5
    }
});
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\pages\MyDrivePage.jsx =====
import React, { useMemo, useCallback, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { FlatList, Text, ActivityIndicator, View, Pressable, Alert, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

import { useMyDrive } from "../hooks/useMyDrive";
import DriveListItem from "../components/DriveListItem";
// ג ScreenLayout ׳”׳•׳¡׳¨ ׳׳›׳׳

import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";
import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { moveToTrash } from "../services/fileService"; 
import { emitDriveRefresh, subscribeNavigateToFolder } from "../services/driveEvents";

import FilePreviewModal from "../components/FilePreviewModal";
export default function MyDrivePage() {
  const [currentFolderId, setCurrentFolderId] = useState(null);
  const [folderPath, setFolderPath] = useState([]);

  const { token } = useAuth();
  const { items, loading, refreshing, onRefresh } = useMyDrive();
  const { colors } = useTheme(); 

  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null);

  // ג… ׳”׳׳–׳ ׳” ׳׳׳™׳¨׳•׳¢ ׳ ׳™׳•׳•׳˜ ׳׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳” (׳׳”׳—׳™׳₪׳•׳©)
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeNavigateToFolder(({ folderId, folderName }) => {
      if (folderId) {
        setCurrentFolderId(folderId);
        setFolderPath([{ id: folderId, name: folderName }]);
      }
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, []);

  // ג… ׳¡׳™׳ ׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳¨׳™׳˜׳™׳ ׳׳₪׳™ ׳”׳×׳™׳§׳™׳™׳” ׳”׳ ׳•׳›׳—׳™׳×
  const currentItems = useMemo(() => {
    if (!items) return [];
    return items.filter(item => {
        if (currentFolderId === null) return !item.parentId;
        return item.parentId === currentFolderId;
    });
  }, [items, currentFolderId]);

  const handlePressItem = useCallback((item) => {
    if (!item) return;
    if (item.type === "folder") {
      setCurrentFolderId(item.id);
      setFolderPath((prev) => [...prev, { id: item.id, name: item.name }]);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedId(item.id);
    setPreviewOpen(true);
  }, []);

  const handleDeleteItem = useCallback(async (item) => {
    Alert.alert("Move to Trash?", `Delete "${item.name}"?`, [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      {
        text: "Delete", style: "destructive",
        onPress: async () => {
          try { await moveToTrash(token, item.id); emitDriveRefresh(); } 
          catch (err) { Alert.alert("Error", "Could not delete item."); }
        }
      }
    ]);
  }, [token]);

  const handleBack = useCallback(() => {
    if (folderPath.length === 0) return;
    const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, -1);
    setFolderPath(newPath);
    setCurrentFolderId(newPath.length > 0 ? newPath[newPath.length - 1].id : null);
  }, [folderPath]);

  const navigateToBreadcrumb = useCallback((index) => {
    if (index < 0) {
      setFolderPath([]);
      setCurrentFolderId(null);
    } else {
      const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, index + 1);
      setFolderPath(newPath);
      setCurrentFolderId(newPath[newPath.length - 1].id);
    }
  }, [folderPath]);

  if (loading && (!currentItems || currentItems.length === 0)) {
    return (
        <View style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
        </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}> 
      {/* Breadcrumbs */}
      {folderPath.length > 0 && (
        <View style={styles.breadcrumbContainer}>
          <Pressable onPress={handleBack} style={[styles.backButton, { backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" }]}>
            <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.textPrimary} />
          </Pressable>
          <Pressable onPress={() => navigateToBreadcrumb(-1)}>
            <Text style={[styles.breadcrumbText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>My Drive</Text>
          </Pressable>
          {folderPath.map((folder, index) => (
            <View key={folder.id} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={14} color={colors.textTertiary} />
                <Pressable onPress={() => navigateToBreadcrumb(index)}>
                    <Text style={[
                        styles.breadcrumbText,
                        { color: index === folderPath.length - 1 ? colors.primary : colors.textSecondary, fontWeight: index === folderPath.length - 1 ? '700' : '400' }
                    ]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {folder.name}
                    </Text>
                </Pressable>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      )}

      <FlatList
        data={currentItems}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 150, paddingHorizontal: 15 }}
        keyExtractor={(item) => item.id || item._id}
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <DriveListItem
            item={item}
            onPress={handlePressItem}
            onDelete={handleDeleteItem}
          />
        )}
        refreshing={refreshing}
        onRefresh={onRefresh}
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 100, opacity: 0.6 }}>
             <Ionicons name="folder-open-outline" size={60} color={colors.textTertiary} />
             <Text style={{ marginTop: 10, color: colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 16 }}>
                {currentFolderId ? "This folder is empty." : "No files found in your drive."}
             </Text>
          </View>
        }
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      />

      <FilePreviewModal
        open={previewOpen}
        fileId={selectedId}
        onClose={() => setPreviewOpen(false)}
      />
    </View>
  );
}
