import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import {
  FlatList,
  Pressable,
  View,
  Text,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Image,
  StyleSheet,
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

import { useMyDrive } from "../hooks/useMyDrive";
import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext";
import DriveListItem from "../components/DriveListItem";
import FilePreviewModal from "../components/FilePreviewModal";
import { getUserById } from "../../user/services/userService";

export default function SharedPage() {
  const { items, loading } = useMyDrive();
  const { user, token } = useAuth();
  const { colors } = useTheme();

  const [ownersData, setOwnersData] = useState({});
  const [ownersLoading, setOwnersLoading] = useState(false);
  const [selectedOwnerId, setSelectedOwnerId] = useState(null);
  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null);

  const myUserId = user?.id || user?._id;
  const ownersCacheRef = useRef({});
  const ownersLoadingRef = useRef(new Set());

  const sharedItems = useMemo(() => {
    return (items || []).filter(
      (f) => f?.ownerId && String(f.ownerId) !== String(myUserId)
    );
  }, [items, myUserId]);

  const uniqueOwnerIds = useMemo(() => {
    const ids = sharedItems.map((f) => String(f.ownerId)).filter(Boolean);
    return Array.from(new Set(ids));
  }, [sharedItems]);

  const loadOwners = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!token || uniqueOwnerIds.length === 0) return;

    const missing = uniqueOwnerIds.filter(
      (id) => !ownersCacheRef.current[id] && !ownersLoadingRef.current.has(id)
    );

    if (missing.length === 0) return;

    missing.forEach((id) => ownersLoadingRef.current.add(id));
    setOwnersLoading(true);

    try {
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(
        missing.map((id) => getUserById(token, id))
      );

      const next = {};
      results.forEach((r, idx) => {
        const id = missing[idx];
        if (r.status === "fulfilled" && r.value) {
          const u = r.value;
          next[id] = {
            id,
            email: u.email || "",
            firstName: u.firstName || "",
            lastName: u.lastName || "",
            image: u.image || u.profilePicture || null,
          };
        } else {
          next[id] = { id, email: "Unknown", firstName: "", lastName: "", image: null };
        }
        ownersLoadingRef.current.delete(id);
      });

      ownersCacheRef.current = { ...ownersCacheRef.current, ...next };
      setOwnersData({ ...ownersCacheRef.current });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("loadOwners error:", e);
    } finally {
      setOwnersLoading(false);
    }
  }, [token, uniqueOwnerIds]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadOwners();
  }, [loadOwners]);

  const ownersList = useMemo(() => {
    return uniqueOwnerIds.map((id) => {
      const o = ownersData[id] || {};
      const fullName = `${o.firstName || ""} ${o.lastName || ""}`.trim();
      const displayName = fullName || (o.email && o.email.includes("@") ? o.email.split("@")[0] : "Shared User");

      return {
        id,
        title: `Shared by ${displayName}`,
        email: o.email || "Unknown",
        image: o.image || null,
      };
    });
  }, [uniqueOwnerIds, ownersData]);

  const filesByOwner = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedOwnerId) return [];
    return sharedItems.filter((f) => String(f.ownerId) === String(selectedOwnerId));
  }, [sharedItems, selectedOwnerId]);

  const openItem = useCallback((item) => {
    if (!item) return;
    if (item.type === "folder") {
      Alert.alert("CopaDrive", "Folders view is coming soon.");
      return;
    }
    setSelectedId(item.id || item._id);
    setPreviewOpen(true);
  }, []);

  if (loading) {
    return <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: 50 }} />;
  }

  if (selectedOwnerId) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <View style={styles.headerContainer}>
          <Pressable onPress={() => setSelectedOwnerId(null)} style={styles.backBtn}>
            <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color="#fff" />
            <Text style={styles.backText}>Back to Users</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <FlatList
          data={filesByOwner}
          keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id || item._id)}
          renderItem={({ item }) => <DriveListItem item={item} onPress={openItem} />}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 150 }}
        />

        <FilePreviewModal open={previewOpen} fileId={selectedId} onClose={() => setPreviewOpen(false)} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      {ownersLoading && <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colors.primary} style={{ marginTop: 10 }} />}

      <FlatList
        data={ownersList}
        keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <Pressable
            style={styles.userCard}
            onPress={() => setSelectedOwnerId(item.id)}
          >
            <View style={styles.avatarWrapper}>
              {item.image ? (
                <Image source={{ uri: item.image }} style={styles.avatar} />
              ) : (
                <View style={[styles.avatar, styles.avatarPlaceholder]}>
                  <Ionicons name="person" size={18} color="#fff" />
                </View>
              )}
            </View>

            <View style={styles.userInfo}>
              <Text style={styles.userName} numberOfLines={1}>
                {item.title}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.userEmail} numberOfLines={1}>
                {item.email}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color="rgba(255,255,255,0.5)" />
          </Pressable>
        )}
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: colors.textTertiary }]}>No shared items yet</Text>
        }
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 150, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingTop: 10 }}
      />

      <FilePreviewModal open={previewOpen} fileId={selectedId} onClose={() => setPreviewOpen(false)} />
    </>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  userCard: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingVertical: 14,
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
    borderRadius: 20,
    marginBottom: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)",
  },
  headerContainer: {
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    marginTop: 10,
    marginBottom: 10,
  },
  backBtn: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
    paddingVertical: 10,
    paddingHorizontal: 15,
    borderRadius: 25,
    alignSelf: 'flex-start',
  },
  backText: {
    fontWeight: "600",
    marginLeft: 8,
    color: "#fff",
    fontSize: 14
  },
  avatarWrapper: { marginRight: 15 },
  avatar: {
    width: 44,

    borderRadius: 22,

  },
  avatarPlaceholder: {
    justifyContent: "center",

import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

import { useMyDrive } from "../hooks/useMyDrive";

export default function StarredPage() {
  const [currentFolderId, setCurrentFolderId] = useState(null);
  const [folderPath, setFolderPath] = useState([]);

  const { token } = useAuth();
  const { items, loading, refreshing, onRefresh } = useMyDrive();
  const { colors } = useTheme(); 

  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null);

  // ׳¡׳™׳ ׳•׳ ׳›׳•׳›׳‘׳™׳
  const displayItems = useMemo(() => {
    if (!currentFolderId) {
        return (items || []).filter((i) => !!i?.starred);
    }
    return (items || []).filter((item) => String(item.parentId) === String(currentFolderId));
  }, [items, currentFolderId]);

  const openItem = useCallback((item) => {
    if (!item) return;
    if (item.type === "folder") {
      setCurrentFolderId(item.id);
      setFolderPath((prev) => [...prev, { id: item.id, name: item.name }]);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedId(item.id);
    setPreviewOpen(true);
  }, []);

      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },

        text: "Delete", style: "destructive",

          try { await moveToTrash(token, item.id); emitDriveRefresh(); } 
          catch (err) { Alert.alert("Error", "Could not delete item."); }
        }
      }
    ]);
  }, [token]);

    if (folderPath.length === 0) return;

    setCurrentFolderId(newPath.length > 0 ? newPath[newPath.length - 1].id : null);
  }, [folderPath]);

    if (index < 0) {
      setFolderPath([]);
      setCurrentFolderId(null);
    } else {
      const newPath = folderPath.slice(0, index + 1);
      setFolderPath(newPath);
      setCurrentFolderId(newPath[newPath.length - 1].id);
    }
  }, [folderPath]);

  if (loading && (!displayItems || displayItems.length === 0)) {

        </View>

      {/* ׳₪׳™׳¨׳•׳¨׳™ ׳׳—׳ */}
      {folderPath.length > 0 && (
        <View style={styles.breadcrumbContainer}>
          <Pressable onPress={handleBack} style={[styles.backButton, { backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.1)" }]}>

          </Pressable>

            <Text style={[styles.breadcrumbText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Starred</Text>
          </Pressable>
          {folderPath.map((folder, index) => (
            <View key={folder.id} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}>

===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\services\driveEvents.js =====
import { DeviceEventEmitter } from "react-native";

const EVENT_NAME = "COPADRIVE_REFRESH";
const NAVIGATE_FOLDER_EVENT = "COPADRIVE_NAVIGATE_FOLDER";

export function emitDriveRefresh(payload = {}) {
  DeviceEventEmitter.emit(EVENT_NAME, payload);
}

export function subscribeDriveRefresh(handler) {
  const sub = DeviceEventEmitter.addListener(EVENT_NAME, handler);
  return () => sub.remove();
}

// Navigate to a specific folder in MyDrive
export function emitNavigateToFolder(folderId, folderName) {
  DeviceEventEmitter.emit(NAVIGATE_FOLDER_EVENT, { folderId, folderName });
}

export function subscribeNavigateToFolder(handler) {
  const sub = DeviceEventEmitter.addListener(NAVIGATE_FOLDER_EVENT, handler);
  return () => sub.remove();
}

import { request } from "../../../shared_code/hooks/api/request";

export async function getFiles(token) {
  return request("/api/files", {
    method: "GET",
    token,
  });
}

// ׳”׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳” ׳”׳–׳• ׳§׳¨׳™׳˜׳™׳× ׳׳”׳•׳¨׳“׳” - ׳”׳™׳ ׳׳‘׳™׳׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳׳™׳“׳¢ ׳”׳׳׳ ׳›׳•׳׳ ׳”-Content (Base64)
export async function getFileById(token, fileId) {
  return request(`/api/files/${fileId}`, {
    method: "GET",
    token,
  });
}

export async function updateItem(token, fileId, patch) {
  return request(`/api/files/${fileId}`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token,
    body: patch, 
  });
}

export async function createItem(
  token,
  { name, type, parentId = null, content = "", mimeType = "" }
) {
  return request("/api/files", {
    method: "POST",
    token,
    body: {
      name,
      type,
      parentId,
      content: type === "file" ? content : "",
      mimeType: type === "file" ? mimeType : "",
    },
  });
}

export async function createFile(token, fileData) {
  // fileData: { name, content, mimeType, parentId? }
  return createItem(token, { ...fileData, type: "file" });
}

export async function createFolder(token, name, parentId = null) {
  return createItem(token, { name, type: "folder", parentId });
}

export async function getUsage(token) {
  return request("/api/files/usage", {
    method: "GET",
    token: token
  });
}

export async function getRecentFiles(token) {
  return request("/api/files/recent", { method: "GET", token });
}

export async function getImportantFiles(token) {
  return request("/api/files/important", { method: "GET", token });
}

export async function getTrashFiles(token) {
  return request("/api/files/trash", { method: "GET", token });
}

export async function moveToTrash(token, fileId) {
  return request(`/api/files/${fileId}/trash`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token,
    body: { trashed: true },
  });
}

export async function restoreFromTrash(token, fileId) {
  return request(`/api/files/${fileId}/trash`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token: token,
    body: { trashed: false },
  });
}

export async function deleteForever(token, fileId) {
  return request(`/api/files/${fileId}/forever`, { method: "DELETE", token });
}

export async function emptyTrash(token) {
  return request("/api/files/trash/empty", { method: "DELETE", token });
}

export async function setStarred(token, fileId, starred) {
  return request(`/api/files/${fileId}/star`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token,
    body: { starred: !!starred },
  });
}

export async function setImportant(token, fileId, important) {
  return request(`/api/files/${fileId}/important`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token,
    body: { important: !!important },
  });
}

export async function renameItem(token, fileId, name) {
  return request(`/api/files/${fileId}`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token,
    body: { name },
  });
}

export function getDaysLeft(trashedAt) {
  if (!trashedAt) return "";
  const trashDate = new Date(trashedAt);
  const deleteDate = new Date(trashDate.getTime() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const now = new Date();
  const diffTime = deleteDate - now;
  const diffDays = Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  return diffDays > 0 ? `${diffDays} days left` : "Deleting soon";
}

export function formatLastAccessed(dateString) {
  if (!dateString) return "Never";
  const date = new Date(dateString);
  const diffMs = new Date() - date;
  const diffMins = Math.floor(diffMs / 60000);

  if (diffMins < 1) return "Just now";
  if (diffMins < 60) return `${diffMins} mins ago`;
  if (diffMs < 86400000) return `${Math.floor(diffMs / 3600000)} hours ago`;
  return date.toLocaleDateString();
}

// mobile-client/src/features/drive/services/permissionService.js

import { request } from "../../../shared_code/hooks/api/request";

/** Get all permissions for a file */
export async function getPermissions(token, fileId) {
    try {
        const response = await request(`/api/files/${fileId}/permissions`, {
            method: "GET",
            token,
        });

        console.log("=== PERMISSIONS SERVICE DEBUG ===");
        console.log("Raw response:", JSON.stringify(response, null, 2));

        // Server might return: { myRole: "owner", permissions: [...] }
        // OR just an array [...]

        let permissionsList = [];

        if (Array.isArray(response)) {
            // Direct array response
            permissionsList = response;
            console.log("Response is direct array");
        } else if (response && Array.isArray(response.permissions)) {
            // Nested in permissions property
            permissionsList = response.permissions;
            console.log("Response has permissions property");
        } else {
            console.log("Unexpected response format");
            return [];
        }

        console.log("Permissions list:", JSON.stringify(permissionsList, null, 2));

        // Map the fields to match what ShareModal expects
        const mapped = permissionsList.map(perm => {
            console.log("Mapping permission:", JSON.stringify(perm, null, 2));

            return {
                id: perm.id || perm._id,
                userId: perm.userId,
                email: perm.email || "",
                firstName: perm.firstName || "",
                lastName: perm.lastName || "",
                image: perm.image || null,
                role: perm.role || "viewer"
            };
        });

        console.log("Mapped permissions:", JSON.stringify(mapped, null, 2));
        console.log("=================================");

        return mapped;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("getPermissions error:", error);
        throw error;
    }
}

/** Add permission (share with user) */
export async function addPermission(token, fileId, userId, role = "viewer") {
    return request(`/api/files/${fileId}/permissions`, {
        method: "POST",
        token,
        body: { userId, role },
    });
}

/** Update permission role */
export async function updatePermission(token, fileId, permissionId, role) {
    return request(`/api/files/${fileId}/permissions/${permissionId}`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        token,
        body: { role },
    });
}

/** Delete permission (revoke access) */
export async function deletePermission(token, fileId, permissionId) {
    return request(`/api/files/${fileId}/permissions/${permissionId}`, {
        method: "DELETE",
        token,
    });
}

/** Find user by email (for sharing) */
export async function findUserByEmail(token, email) {
    const safe = encodeURIComponent(String(email).trim());
    return request(`/api/users/email/${safe}`, {
        method: "GET",
        token,
    });
}
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\services\searchService.js =====
import { request } from "../../../shared_code/hooks/api/request";

export async function fetchSearchItems(token, query) {
    if (!query || query.trim().length === 0) return [];

    try {
        const encoded = encodeURIComponent(query.trim());
        const data = await request(`/api/search/${encoded}`, {
            method: "GET",
            token: token,
        });
        return Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Search failed:", error.message);
        return [];
    }
}

/**
 * ׳‘-JavaScript ׳׳™׳ interfaces.
 * ׳”׳”׳’׳“׳¨׳” ׳׳׳˜׳” ׳”׳™׳ JSDoc ׳©׳¢׳•׳–׳¨׳× ׳-VS Code ׳׳”׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳‘׳ ׳”,

===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\utils\downloadFile.js =====
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import * as Sharing from "expo-sharing";

function getExtensionFromMimeType(mimeType) {
  if (!mimeType) return "";
  const mappings = {
    "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
    "image/png": ".png",
    "image/gif": ".gif",
    "application/pdf": ".pdf",
    "video/mp4": ".mp4",
    "audio/mpeg": ".mp3",
    "audio/wav": ".wav",
    "text/plain": ".txt",
    "application/json": ".json",
    "text/javascript": ".js",
    "text/html": ".html",
    "text/css": ".css"
  };
  return mappings[mimeType] || "";
}

// ׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳™׳× ׳¢׳–׳¨ ׳׳ ׳™׳§׳•׳™ Base64 ׳׳§׳™׳“׳•׳׳•׳× ׳›׳׳• data:image/png;base64,
function cleanBase64(content) {
  if (typeof content === 'string' && content.includes(';base64,')) {
    return content.split(';base64,')[1];
  }
  return content;
}

// ג… ׳©׳™׳ ׳™׳×׳™ ׳׳× ׳”׳©׳ ׳-downloadFile (׳›׳׳׳™ ׳™׳•׳×׳¨)
// ג… ׳”׳•׳¡׳₪׳×׳™ ׳₪׳¨׳׳˜׳¨ isBase64
export async function downloadFile({ content, mimeType, fileName, isBase64 = true }) {
  if (!content) throw new Error("No content to download");

  let cleanName = (fileName || "downloaded_file").replace(/[\/\\?%*:|"<>]/g, "_");

  // ׳”׳•׳¡׳₪׳× ׳¡׳™׳•׳׳× ׳׳ ׳—׳¡׳¨׳”
  if (!cleanName.includes(".")) {
    const ext = getExtensionFromMimeType(mimeType);
    cleanName += ext;
  }

  // ׳•׳™׳“׳•׳ ׳©׳”-Cache ׳§׳™׳™׳
  if (!FileSystem.cacheDirectory) {
      throw new Error("FileSystem.cacheDirectory is undefined.");
  }
  
  const uri = FileSystem.cacheDirectory + cleanName;

  try {
    if (isBase64) {
        // ג… ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳”/PDF: ׳׳ ׳§׳™׳ ׳•׳›׳•׳×׳‘׳™׳ ׳›-Base64
        const rawData = cleanBase64(content);
        await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(uri, rawData, {
            encoding: 'base64', 
        });
    } else {
        // ג… ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳˜׳§׳¡׳˜: ׳›׳•׳×׳‘׳™׳ ׳›׳˜׳§׳¡׳˜ ׳¨׳’׳™׳ (UTF-8)
        // ׳׳ ׳”׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳•׳‘׳™׳™׳§׳˜ (׳׳׳©׳ JSON), ׳ ׳”׳₪׳•׳ ׳׳׳—׳¨׳•׳–׳×
        const textData = typeof content === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(content, null, 2) : String(content);
        await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(uri, textData);
    }

    const canShare = await Sharing.isAvailableAsync();
    if (canShare) {
      await Sharing.shareAsync(uri, {
        mimeType: mimeType || "application/octet-stream",
        dialogTitle: `Download ${cleanName}`,
        UTI: mimeType 
      });
    } else {
      throw new Error("Sharing is not available on this device");
    }

    return uri;

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("DownloadFile Error:", error);
    throw error;
  }
}
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\utils\fileIcon.js =====
// mobile-client/src/features/drive/utils/fileIcon.js

/**
 * Returns icon meta (Ionicons name + color) by file name/mime + folder flag
 */
export function getFileIconMeta(name, mimeType, isFolder) {
  if (isFolder) return { icon: "folder", color: "#f8d775" };

  const safeName = String(name || "");
  const ext = safeName.includes(".") ? safeName.split(".").pop().toLowerCase() : "";
  const mime = String(mimeType || "").toLowerCase();

  // Documents
  if (["doc", "docx", "odt"].includes(ext) || mime.includes("word"))
    return { icon: "document-text", color: "#4285f4" };

  // Spreadsheets
  if (["xls", "xlsx", "csv", "ods"].includes(ext) || mime.includes("spreadsheet") || mime.includes("excel"))
    return { icon: "grid", color: "#34a853" };

  // Presentations
  if (["ppt", "pptx", "odp"].includes(ext) || mime.includes("presentation") || mime.includes("powerpoint"))
    return { icon: "easel", color: "#f4b400" };

  // PDF
  if (ext === "pdf" || mime === "application/pdf")
    return { icon: "document", color: "#ea4335" };

  // Images
  if (["jpg", "jpeg", "png", "gif", "svg", "webp", "heic"].includes(ext) || mime.startsWith("image/"))
    return { icon: "image", color: "#ea4335" };

  // Video
  if (["mp4", "mkv", "avi", "mov"].includes(ext) || mime.startsWith("video/"))
    return { icon: "play-circle", color: "#ea4335" };

  // Audio
  if (["mp3", "wav", "ogg", "m4a"].includes(ext) || mime.startsWith("audio/"))
    return { icon: "musical-notes", color: "#a142f4" };

  // Archives
  if (["zip", "rar", "7z", "tar", "gz"].includes(ext) || mime.includes("zip") || mime.includes("compressed"))
    return { icon: "archive", color: "#fbbc04" };

  // Code
  if (["js", "jsx", "ts", "tsx", "html", "css", "json", "py", "java", "c", "cpp"].includes(ext))
    return { icon: "code-slash", color: "#4285f4" };

  // Text
  if (ext === "txt" || mime === "text/plain" || mime.startsWith("text/"))
    return { icon: "document-text", color: "#4285f4" };

  return { icon: "document-outline", color: "#9aa0a6" };
}
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\drive\utils\fileType.js =====
export const isTextFile = (mime = "", name = "") => {
  const n = (name || "").toLowerCase();
  return (
    mime === "text/plain" ||
    mime.startsWith("text/") ||
    mime === "application/json" ||
    mime === "application/javascript" ||
    mime === "application/xml" ||
    n.endsWith(".txt")
  );
};

export const isImageFile = (mime = "", name = "") => {
  const n = (name || "").toLowerCase();
  return (
    (mime || "").startsWith("image/") ||
    /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif|bmp|webp)$/i.test(n)
  );
};

export const isPdfFile = (mime = "", name = "") => {
  const n = (name || "").toLowerCase();
  return mime === "application/pdf" || n.endsWith(".pdf");
};

export const isVideoFile = (mime = "", name = "") => {
  const n = (name || "").toLowerCase();
  return (mime || "").startsWith("video/") || /\.(mp4|mov|mkv|webm)$/i.test(n);
};

export const canEditContent = (item) => {
  if (!item || item.type !== "file") return false;
  return isTextFile(item.mimeType, item.name);
};
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\features\user\components\EditProfileModal.jsx =====
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Modal,
  Platform,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
  Image,
  Dimensions
} from "react-native";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

import { useAuth } from "../../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { updateUserProfile, changeUserPassword } from "../services/userService";
import { useTheme } from "../../../app/providers/ThemeContext"; // ג… Theme

const { height: SCREEN_HEIGHT } = Dimensions.get("window");

const formatIsraeliPhone = (raw) => {
  let v = String(raw || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (v.length > 10) v = v.slice(0, 10);
  if (v.length <= 3) return v;
  if (v.length <= 6) return `${v.slice(0, 3)}-${v.slice(3)}`;
  return `${v.slice(0, 3)}-${v.slice(3, 6)}-${v.slice(6)}`;
};

export default function EditProfileModal({
  visible,
  onClose,
  userId,
  initialFirstName = "",
  initialLastName = "",
  initialPhoneNumber = "",
  initialImage = "",
}) {
  const { token } = useAuth();
  const { colors, isDarkMode } = useTheme(); // ג…

  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState(initialFirstName);
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState(initialLastName);
  const [phoneNumber, setPhoneNumber] = useState(formatIsraeliPhone(initialPhoneNumber));
  const [image, setImage] = useState(initialImage);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  // Password fields
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmNewPassword, setConfirmNewPassword] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    setFirstName(initialFirstName);
    setLastName(initialLastName);
    setPhoneNumber(formatIsraeliPhone(initialPhoneNumber));
    setImage(initialImage);
    setCurrentPassword("");
    setNewPassword("");
    setConfirmNewPassword("");
  }, [visible, initialFirstName, initialLastName, initialPhoneNumber, initialImage]);

  const handlePickImage = async () => {
    const { status } = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
    if (status !== 'granted') {
      Alert.alert("Permission Required", "Please allow access to photos.");
      return;
    }
    let result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
      allowsEditing: true,
      aspect: [1, 1],
      quality: 0.8,
    });
    if (!result.canceled) setImage(result.assets[0].uri);
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    // ג… ׳•׳׳™׳“׳¦׳™׳” ׳׳©׳
    if (!firstName.trim()) {
      Alert.alert("Validation Error", "First name is a required field.");
      return;
    }

    // ג… ׳•׳׳™׳“׳¦׳™׳” ׳׳˜׳׳₪׳•׳ (׳‘׳“׳™׳•׳§ ׳›׳׳• ׳‘׳”׳¨׳©׳׳”)
    const rawPhone = phoneNumber.replace(/\D/g, "");
    if (phoneNumber.length > 0 && rawPhone.length < 10) {
        Alert.alert("Validation Error", "Phone number must be 10 digits.");
        return;
    }

    // ג… ׳•׳׳™׳“׳¦׳™׳” ׳׳¡׳™׳¡׳׳”
    if (currentPassword || newPassword || confirmNewPassword) {
        if (!currentPassword) {
            Alert.alert("Validation Error", "Please enter your current password.");
            return;
        }
        if (!newPassword) {
            Alert.alert("Validation Error", "Please enter a new password.");
            return;
        }
        if (newPassword.length < 6) {
            Alert.alert("Validation Error", "New password must be at least 6 characters.");
            return;
        }
        if (newPassword === currentPassword) {
            Alert.alert("Validation Error", "New password cannot be the same as the old one.");
            return;
        }
        if (newPassword !== confirmNewPassword) {
            Alert.alert("Validation Error", "New passwords do not match.");
            return;
        }
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const patch = { 
          firstName: firstName.trim(), 
          lastName: lastName.trim(), 
          phoneNumber: phoneNumber.trim(), 
          image 
      };
      await updateUserProfile(token, userId, patch);

      if (currentPassword && newPassword) {
        await changeUserPassword(token, userId, currentPassword, newPassword);
      }
      onClose?.();
    } catch (e) {
      Alert.alert("Error", e.message || "Update failed");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade">
      <View style={styles.overlay}>
        {/* ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳׳˜׳•׳©׳˜׳© */}
        <BlurView intensity={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 40 : 80} tint="dark" style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />

        <KeyboardAvoidingView
          behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : "height"}
          style={styles.keyboardWrapper}
        >
          <Pressable 
            style={[
                styles.card, 
                { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "#1e1e1e" : "#fff" } // ג… ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™
            ]} 
            onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Edit profile</Text>

            <ScrollView
              style={styles.scrollArea}
              contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
              keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            >
              {/* ׳×׳׳•׳ ׳× ׳₪׳¨׳•׳₪׳™׳ */}
              <View style={styles.imageSection}>
                <Pressable onPress={handlePickImage} style={styles.avatarWrapper}>
                  {image ? (
                    <Image source={{ uri: image }} style={[styles.avatar, { borderColor: colors.border }]} />
                  ) : (
                    <View style={[styles.avatar, styles.avatarPlaceholder, { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                      <Ionicons name="person" size={50} color={colors.textTertiary} />
                    </View>
                  )}
                  <View style={[styles.cameraIconBadge, { borderColor: isDarkMode ? "#1e1e1e" : "#fff" }]}>
                    <Ionicons name="camera" size={20} color="#fff" />
                  </View>
                </Pressable>
                <Text style={styles.changePhotoText}>Tap to change photo</Text>
              </View>

              {/* ׳©׳“׳•׳× ׳˜׳§׳¡׳˜ */}
              <Field 
                label="FIRST NAME" 
                value={firstName} 
                onChangeText={setFirstName} 
                colors={colors} 
                isDarkMode={isDarkMode} 
              />
              <Field 
                label="LAST NAME" 
                value={lastName} 
                onChangeText={setLastName} 
                colors={colors} 
                isDarkMode={isDarkMode} 
              />
              <Field
                label="PHONE NUMBER"
                value={phoneNumber}
                onChangeText={(t) => setPhoneNumber(formatIsraeliPhone(t))}
                keyboardType="phone-pad"
                colors={colors} 
                isDarkMode={isDarkMode} 
              />

              <View style={[styles.divider, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />

              <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Security Settings</Text>
              
              <Field 
                label="CURRENT PASSWORD" 
                value={currentPassword} 
                onChangeText={setCurrentPassword} 
                secureTextEntry 
                colors={colors} 
                isDarkMode={isDarkMode} 
              />
              <Field 
                label="NEW PASSWORD" 
                value={newPassword} 
                onChangeText={setNewPassword} 
                secureTextEntry 
                placeholder="" 
                colors={colors} 
                isDarkMode={isDarkMode} 
              />
              <Field 
                label="CONFIRM NEW PASSWORD" 
                value={confirmNewPassword} 
                onChangeText={setConfirmNewPassword} 
                secureTextEntry 
                colors={colors} 
                isDarkMode={isDarkMode} 
              />

            </ScrollView>

            {/* ׳›׳₪׳×׳•׳¨׳™׳ */}
            <View style={[styles.actions, { borderTopColor: colors.border }]}>
              <TouchableOpacity 
                style={[styles.btn, { backgroundColor: isDarkMode ? "#333" : "#f1f3f4" }]} 
                onPress={onClose}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.btnTextSecondary, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Cancel</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              
              <TouchableOpacity 
                style={[styles.btn, styles.btnPrimary]} 
                onPress={handleSave} 
                disabled={saving}
              >
                {saving ? (
                    <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
                ) : (
                    <Text style={styles.btnText}>Save</Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </Pressable>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function Field({ label, colors, isDarkMode, ...props }) {

  );
}

import { request } from "../../../shared_code/hooks/api/request";

/** ׳©׳׳™׳₪׳× ׳₪׳¨׳•׳₪׳™׳ ׳׳©׳×׳׳© ׳׳₪׳™ ID */
export async function getUserProfile(token, userId) {
  return request(`/api/users/${userId}`, {
    method: "GET",
    token: token
  });
}

export async function updateUserProfile(token, userId, patch) {
  return request(`/api/users/${userId}`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token: token,
    body: patch,
  });
}

export async function changeUserPassword(token, userId, currentPassword, newPassword) {
  return request(`/api/users/${userId}/password`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token: token,
    body: { currentPassword, newPassword },
  });
}

export async function getUserById(token, userId) {
  return request(`/api/users/${userId}`, {
    method: "GET",
    token,
  });
}

import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity,
  StyleSheet, ScrollView, Platform, InputAccessoryView, Button, Keyboard
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";

const ACCESSORY_ID = "fab_create_content_done";

export default function CreateForm({ 
  mode, // 'create_folder' or 'create_file'
  name, setName, 
  content, setContent, 
  folders = [],
  selectedFolderId, setSelectedFolderId,
  onSubmit, onBack, loading 
}) {
  const isFolder = mode === "create_folder";
  const [showFolderPicker, setShowFolderPicker] = useState(false);

  const selectedFolderName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedFolderId) return "My Drive (Root)";
    const folder = folders?.find(f => f.id === selectedFolderId);
    return folder?.name || "Unknown Folder";
  }, [selectedFolderId, folders]);

  return (
    <View style={styles.centerContainer}>
      <View style={styles.modalCard}>
        <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>
          {isFolder ? "CREATE NEW FOLDER" : "CREATE NEW FILE"}
        </Text>

        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          placeholder={isFolder ? "Folder Name" : "File Name"}
          value={name}
          onChangeText={setName}
          autoFocus
          returnKeyType="done"
          onSubmitEditing={() => Keyboard.dismiss()}
        />

        {!isFolder && (
          <ScrollView style={{ width: "100%" }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
            <TextInput
              style={[styles.input, { height: 120, textAlignVertical: "top" }]}
              placeholder="Content..."
              value={content}
              onChangeText={setContent}
              multiline
              inputAccessoryViewID={ACCESSORY_ID}
            />
          </ScrollView>
        )}

        {/* Folder Picker */}
        <Text style={styles.label}>Save to</Text>
        <TouchableOpacity 
          style={styles.folderPicker} 
          onPress={() => setShowFolderPicker(!showFolderPicker)}
        >
          <Ionicons name="folder-outline" size={20} color="#666" />
          <Text style={styles.folderPickerText}>{selectedFolderName}</Text>
          <Ionicons 
            name={showFolderPicker ? "chevron-up" : "chevron-down"} 
            size={18} 
            color="#666" 
          />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {showFolderPicker && (
          <View style={styles.folderList}>
            <ScrollView nestedScrollEnabled keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.folderItem, 
                  !selectedFolderId && styles.folderItemActive
                ]}
                onPress={() => { 
                  setSelectedFolderId(null); 
                  setShowFolderPicker(false); 
                }}
              >
                <Ionicons name="home-outline" size={18} color="#666" />
                <Text style={styles.folderItemText}>My Drive (Root)</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              
              {folders?.filter(f => f.type === "folder").map(folder => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={folder.id}
                  style={[
                    styles.folderItem, 
                    selectedFolderId === folder.id && styles.folderItemActive
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => { 
                    setSelectedFolderId(folder.id); 
                    setShowFolderPicker(false); 
                  }}
                >
                  <Ionicons name="folder" size={18} color="#f59e0b" />
                  <Text style={styles.folderItemText}>{folder.name}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        )}

        {Platform.OS === "ios" && (
          <InputAccessoryView nativeID={ACCESSORY_ID}>
            <View style={styles.accessory}>
              <Button onPress={() => Keyboard.dismiss()} title="Done" />
            </View>
          </InputAccessoryView>
        )}

        <View style={styles.modalButtons}>
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={onBack} disabled={loading}>
            <Text style={styles.cancelText}>BACK</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.createBtn, loading && { opacity: 0.7 }]}
            onPress={onSubmit}
            disabled={loading}
          >
            <Text style={styles.confirmText}>CREATE</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  centerContainer: {
    flex: 1,
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: 30
  },
  modalCard: {
    backgroundColor: "white",
    borderRadius: 24,
    padding: 24,
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 400,
    alignItems: "center",
    elevation: 10,
    shadowColor: "#000",
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.15,
    shadowRadius: 12,
  },
  modalTitle: { fontSize: 18, marginBottom: 20, color: "#333", textAlign: "center", fontWeight: '700' },
  input: {
    width: "100%",
    backgroundColor: "#f8f9fa",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#e9ecef",
    borderRadius: 12,
    padding: 15,
    fontSize: 16,
    marginBottom: 15,
  },
  label: {
    alignSelf: "flex-start",
    fontSize: 14,

  folderPicker: {
    flexDirection: "row",

    borderBottomColor: "#e9ecef",
  },
  folderItemActive: {
    backgroundColor: "#e0f2fe",
  },

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  View, Text, TouchableOpacity, StyleSheet, Modal,
  ActivityIndicator, Pressable, Keyboard, LayoutAnimation, Alert
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";

import { useAuth } from "../../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { createItem, getFiles } from "../../features/drive/services/fileService";
import { emitDriveRefresh } from "../../features/drive/services/driveEvents";
import { useDriveUploads } from "../../features/drive/hooks/useDriveUploads";

import ActionMenu from "./ActionMenu";
import CreateForm from "./CreateForm";
import UploadPreviewContent from "../../features/drive/components/UploadPreviewContent";

// ג… ׳™׳™׳‘׳•׳ Theme
import { useTheme } from "../../app/providers/ThemeContext";

const NEWLINE = "_@NL@_";

export default function Fab({ onRefresh }) {
  const { token } = useAuth();
  
  // ג… ׳©׳׳™׳₪׳× Theme
  const { isDarkMode, colors } = useTheme();

  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState("closed");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [content, setContent] = useState("");
  const [createLoading, setCreateLoading] = useState(false);
  const [folders, setFolders] = useState([]);
  const [selectedFolderId, setSelectedFolderId] = useState(null);

  const closeAll = () => {
    setActiveView("closed");
    setTimeout(() => { setName(""); setContent(""); setSelectedFolderId(null); }, 300);
    Keyboard.dismiss();
  };

  const { 
    loading: uploadLoading, 
    pickDocument, 
    pickImage,
    pendingFile,
    confirmUpload,
    discardPending,
  } = useDriveUploads(token, onRefresh, closeAll);

  // Fetch folders for destination selection
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchFolders = async () => {
      if (!token) return;
      try {
        const files = await getFiles(token);
        const foldersList = (files || []).filter(f => f.type === "folder");
        setFolders(foldersList);
      } catch (err) {
        // Silently fail - folders will just be empty
      }
    };
    fetchFolders();
  }, [token]);

  const switchTo = (view) => {
    LayoutAnimation.configureNext(LayoutAnimation.Presets.easeInEaseOut);
    setActiveView(view);
  };

  const handleMenuAction = (action) => {
    switch(action) {
      case "create_folder": switchTo("create_folder"); break;
      case "create_file":   switchTo("create_file"); break;
      case "upload_image":  pickImage(false); break;
      case "take_photo":    pickImage(true); break;
      case "upload_file":   pickDocument(); break;
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitCreate = async () => {
    if (!name.trim()) return;
    if (!token) return Alert.alert("Error", "Not logged in");

    setCreateLoading(true);
    try {
      const isFolder = activeView === "create_folder";
      const finalName = isFolder ? name.trim() : (name.trim().endsWith(".txt") ? name.trim() : `${name.trim()}.txt`);

      await createItem(token, {
        name: finalName,
        type: isFolder ? "folder" : "file",
        content: isFolder ? "" : String(content || "").replaceAll("\n", NEWLINE),
        mimeType: isFolder ? "" : "text/plain",
        parentId: selectedFolderId,
      });

      emitDriveRefresh({ reason: "create", name: finalName });
      onRefresh?.();
      closeAll();
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Error", err?.message || "Unknown error");
    } finally {
      setCreateLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const loading = uploadLoading || createLoading;
  const isMenu = activeView === "menu";
  const isCreate = activeView === "create_file" || activeView === "create_folder";
  const isPreview = !!pendingFile;
  
  // Modal is open if menu/create view is active OR if there's a pending file to preview
  const isOpen = activeView !== "closed" || isPreview;

  return (
    <>
      {loading && (
        <View style={styles.loaderOverlay}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
          <Text style={styles.loaderText}>WORKING...</Text>
        </View>
      )}

      <Modal visible={isOpen} transparent animationType="fade">
        <View style={styles.container}>
          {/* ג… BlurView ׳׳•׳×׳׳ ׳׳׳™׳׳” */}
          <BlurView intensity={30} tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "light"} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />
          <Pressable style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} onPress={isPreview ? discardPending : closeAll} />

          {isMenu && !isPreview && (
            <ActionMenu onAction={handleMenuAction} disabled={loading} />
          )}

          {isCreate && !isPreview && (
            <CreateForm 
              mode={activeView}
              name={name} setName={setName}
              content={content} setContent={setContent}
              folders={folders}
              selectedFolderId={selectedFolderId}
              setSelectedFolderId={setSelectedFolderId}
              loading={loading}
              onBack={() => switchTo("menu")}
              onSubmit={handleSubmitCreate}
            />
          )}

          {isPreview && (
            <UploadPreviewContent
              fileData={pendingFile}
              folders={folders}
              uploading={uploadLoading}
              onConfirm={(fileName, folderId) => confirmUpload(fileName, folderId)}
              onDiscard={discardPending}
              onChangeFile={() => {
                discardPending();
                // Reopen the menu so user can pick another file
                switchTo("menu");
              }}
            />
          )}
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {!isOpen && (
        <View style={styles.fabPosition}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
                styles.fabMain, 
                { backgroundColor: colors.primary }, // ג… ׳¦׳‘׳¢ ׳›׳₪׳×׳•׳¨ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™
                loading && { opacity: 0.7 }
            ]}
            onPress={() => !loading && switchTo("menu")}
            activeOpacity={0.9}
            disabled={loading}
          >
            <Ionicons name="add" size={35} color="white" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  fabPosition: { position: "absolute", bottom: 90, right: 25, zIndex: 9999 },
  fabMain: {
    width: 60, height: 60, borderRadius: 30,
    justifyContent: "center", alignItems: "center", elevation: 5,
  },
  container: { flex: 1 },
  loaderOverlay: {
    position: "absolute", top: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0,
    backgroundColor: "rgba(0,0,0,0.4)", justifyContent: "center", alignItems: "center", zIndex: 10000,
  },
  loaderText: { marginTop: 15, color: "white", fontSize: 16, letterSpacing: 2, fontWeight: 'bold' },
});
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\layout\SearchBar\SearchBar.jsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { View, Text, TextInput, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, Keyboard } from "react-native";
import SearchDropdown from "./SearchDropdown";
import { fetchSearchItems } from "../../features/drive/services/searchService";
import FilePreviewModal from "../../features/drive/components/FilePreviewModal";
import { emitNavigateToFolder } from "../../features/drive/services/driveEvents";

export default function SearchBar({ token }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [results, setResults] = useState([]);
  const [isFocused, setIsFocused] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [selectedFileId, setSelectedFileId] = useState(null);
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!query.trim()) {
      setResults([]);
      return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      const data = await fetchSearchItems(token, query);
      setResults(data || []);
      setLoading(false);
    }, 200); 
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [query]);

  const handleFileClick = (file) => {
    setQuery("");
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    setIsFocused(false);

    if (file.type === "folder") {
      emitNavigateToFolder(file.id, file.name);
      return;
    }

    setSelectedFileId(file.id);
    setModalOpen(true);
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.inputBar}>
        <Text style={styles.treeIcon}>נ´</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          placeholder={isFocused ? "" : "Search in CopaDrive"}
          placeholderTextColor="rgba(95, 99, 104, 0.5)"
          value={query}
          onChangeText={setQuery}
          onFocus={() => setIsFocused(true)}
          onBlur={() => setTimeout(() => setIsFocused(false), 200)}
        />
        {loading && <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#0d6efd" />}
      </View>

      {isFocused && (query.length > 0 || loading) && (
        <SearchDropdown
          results={results}
          loading={loading}
          query={query}
          onResultPress={handleFileClick} 
        />
      )}

      <FilePreviewModal
        open={modalOpen}
        fileId={selectedFileId}
        onClose={() => {
          setModalOpen(false);
          setSelectedFileId(null);
        }}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, zIndex: 5000 },
  inputBar: { flex: 1, flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", paddingLeft: 5 },
  treeIcon: { fontSize: 20, marginRight: 8 },
  input: { flex: 1, fontSize: 16, color: "#202124", height: "100%" },
});
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\layout\SearchBar\SearchDropdown.jsx =====
import React from "react";
import { FlatList, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { getFileIconMeta } from "../../features/drive/utils/fileIcon";

export default function SearchDropdown({ results, loading, query, onResultPress }) {
    const renderItem = ({ item }) => {
        const { icon, color } = getFileIconMeta(item.name, item.mimeType, item.type === "folder");
        return (
            <Pressable style={styles.item} onPress={() => onResultPress(item)}>
                <Ionicons name={icon} size={22} color={color} style={styles.icon} />
                <Text style={styles.text} numberOfLines={1}>{item.name}</Text>
            </Pressable>
        );
    };

    return (
        <BlurView intensity={85} tint="light" style={styles.dropdown}>
            <View style={styles.overlay} />

            <FlatList
                data={results}
                keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
                keyboardShouldPersistTaps="always"
                renderItem={renderItem}
                ListEmptyComponent={!loading && query.length > 0 && (
                    <Text style={styles.emptyText}>No matches found for "{query}"</Text>
                )}
            />
        </BlurView>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    dropdown: {
        position: "absolute",
        top: 55,
        left: 0,
        right: 0,
        borderRadius: 20,
        maxHeight: 350,
        overflow: "hidden",
        borderWidth: 1,
        borderColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.5)",
    },
    overlay: {
        ...StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject,
        backgroundColor: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4)",
    },
    item: {
        flexDirection: "row",
        alignItems: "center",
        padding: 15,
        borderBottomWidth: 0.5,
        borderBottomColor: "rgba(0,0,0,0.05)",
    },
    icon: { marginRight: 15 },
    text: { fontSize: 16, color: "#202124", flex: 1, fontWeight: "500" },
    emptyText: { padding: 20, textAlign: "center", color: "#70757a" }
});
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\layout\BottomBar.jsx =====
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { 
  View, 
  StyleSheet, 
  Dimensions, 
  Animated, 
  PanResponder 
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";

// ג… ׳™׳™׳‘׳•׳ Theme
import { useTheme } from "../app/providers/ThemeContext";

const { width: SCREEN_WIDTH } = Dimensions.get("window");
const MARGIN_H = 20;
const BAR_WIDTH = SCREEN_WIDTH - (MARGIN_H * 2);
const TAB_COUNT = 4;
const TAB_WIDTH = BAR_WIDTH / TAB_COUNT;
const BUBBLE_WIDTH = TAB_WIDTH * 0.7;
const BUBBLE_OFFSET = (TAB_WIDTH - BUBBLE_WIDTH) / 2;

export default function BottomBar({ activeTab, setActiveTab }) {
  // ג… ׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳‘׳¦׳‘׳¢׳™׳
  const { isDarkMode, colors } = useTheme();

  const slideAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const scaleAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;

  const tabs = ["home", "starred", "shared", "mydrive"];

  const animateToTab = (index) => {
    const targetX = (index * TAB_WIDTH) + BUBBLE_OFFSET;
    Animated.spring(slideAnim, {
      toValue: targetX,
      useNativeDriver: true,
      speed: 15,
      bounciness: 8,
    }).start();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const index = tabs.indexOf(activeTab);
    if (index !== -1) {
      animateToTab(index);
    }
  }, [activeTab]);

  const panResponder = useRef(
    PanResponder.create({
      onStartShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
      onPanResponderGrant: () => {
        Animated.spring(scaleAnim, { toValue: 0.9, useNativeDriver: true }).start();
        slideAnim.stopAnimation((value) => {
           slideAnim.setOffset(value); 
           slideAnim.setValue(0);
        });
      },
      onPanResponderMove: (evt, gestureState) => {
         slideAnim.setValue(gestureState.dx);
      },
      onPanResponderRelease: (evt, gestureState) => {
        Animated.spring(scaleAnim, { toValue: 1, useNativeDriver: true }).start();
        slideAnim.flattenOffset();
        const touchX = evt.nativeEvent.locationX;
        let targetIndex = Math.floor(touchX / TAB_WIDTH);
        if (targetIndex < 0) targetIndex = 0;
        if (targetIndex >= TAB_COUNT) targetIndex = TAB_COUNT - 1;
        const newTab = tabs[targetIndex];
        setActiveTab(newTab);
        animateToTab(targetIndex);
      },
    })
  ).current;

  const NavItem = ({ icon, id }) => {
    const isActive = activeTab === id;
    return (
      <View style={styles.navItem}>
        <View style={styles.iconContainer}>
          <Ionicons 
            name={icon} 
            size={24} 
            // ג… ׳”׳׳™׳™׳§׳•׳ ׳”׳׳ ׳₪׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳¦׳‘׳¢ ׳׳•׳×׳׳ ׳׳׳™׳׳”/׳™׳•׳
            color={isActive ? "#fff" : (isDarkMode ? "#8E8E93" : "#5f6368")} 
          />
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* ג… ׳”-BlurView ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳” ׳׳•׳˜׳•׳׳˜׳™׳× (dark/light) */}
      <BlurView intensity={90} tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "light"} style={styles.blurContainer}>
        
        <View 
          style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} 
          {...panResponder.panHandlers} 
          zIndex={10} 
        />

        <Animated.View 
          style={[
            styles.slidingBubble,
            { backgroundColor: colors.primary }, // ג… ׳”׳‘׳•׳¢׳” ׳‘׳¦׳‘׳¢ ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳™ ׳©׳ ׳”׳ ׳•׳©׳
            {
              transform: [
                { translateX: slideAnim },
                { scale: scaleAnim }
              ] 
            }
          ]} 
        />

        <View style={styles.innerContent} pointerEvents="none"> 
          <NavItem icon="home" id="home" />
          <NavItem icon="star" id="starred" />
          <NavItem icon="people" id="shared" />
          <NavItem icon="folder" id="mydrive" />
        </View>

      </BlurView>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    position: "absolute",
    bottom: 30,
    left: MARGIN_H,
    right: MARGIN_H,
    height: 65,
    borderRadius: 35,
    overflow: "hidden",
    shadowColor: "#000",
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 8 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.15,
    shadowRadius: 20,
    elevation: 8,
  },
  blurContainer: {
    flex: 1,
    justifyContent: "center",
  },
  slidingBubble: {
    position: "absolute",
    width: BUBBLE_WIDTH,
    height: 44,
    // backgroundColor ׳”׳•׳¡׳¨ ׳׳›׳׳ ׳›׳™ ׳”׳•׳ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™
    borderRadius: 22,
    left: 0,
  },
  innerContent: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    height: "100%",
    justifyContent: "space-between",
  },
  navItem: {
    width: TAB_WIDTH,
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  iconContainer: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
});
===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\layout\Fab.jsx =====
import React, { useRef, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  StyleSheet,
  Modal,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Pressable,
  Keyboard,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  FlatList,
} from "react-native";
import { Ionicons } from "@expo/vector-icons";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import * as DocumentPicker from "expo-document-picker";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import { BlurView } from "expo-blur";

import { useAuth } from "../app/providers/AuthContext";
import { createItem } from "../features/drive/services/fileService";
import { emitDriveRefresh } from "../features/drive/services/driveEvents";
import { useMyDrive } from "../features/drive/hooks/useMyDrive";

const NEWLINE = "_@NL@_";
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

export default function Fab({ onRefresh }) {
  const { token } = useAuth();
  const { items } = useMyDrive();

  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [modalType, setModalType] = useState(null); // "createfolder" | "createfile"
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [content, setContent] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [parentId, setParentId] = useState(null);
  const [showFolderPicker, setShowFolderPicker] = useState(false);

  const pickerInProgressRef = useRef(false);

  // Get all folders for the parent dropdown
  const folders = useMemo(() => {
    return (items || []).filter((item) => item.type === "folder");
  }, [items]);

  // Get selected folder name for display
  const selectedFolderName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!parentId) return "Root (My Drive)";
    const folder = folders.find((f) => (f.id || f._id) === parentId);
    return folder?.name || "Selected Folder";
  }, [parentId, folders]);

  const lockPicker = () => {
    if (pickerInProgressRef.current) return false;
    pickerInProgressRef.current = true;
    return true;
  };
  const unlockPicker = () => {
    pickerInProgressRef.current = false;
  };

  const closeMenu = () => {
    setIsOpen(false);
  };

  const closeCreate = () => {
    setModalType(null);
    setName("");
    setContent("");
    setParentId(null);
    setShowFolderPicker(false);
    Keyboard.dismiss();
  };

  const processAndUpload = async (uri, fileName, mimeType) => {
    if (!token) {
      Alert.alert("Not logged in", "No token found.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);

      const base64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(uri, {
        encoding: "base64",
      });

      await createItem(token, {
        name: fileName,
        type: "file",
        content: base64,
        mimeType: mimeType || "application/octet-stream",
      });

      emitDriveRefresh({ reason: "upload", name: fileName });
      onRefresh?.();
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Upload Failed", err?.message || "Unknown error");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      unlockPicker();
    }
  };

  const handlePickDocument = async () => {
    if (!lockPicker()) return;

    try {
      closeMenu();
      await wait(200);

      const result = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({
        copyToCacheDirectory: true,
        type: "*/*",
        multiple: false,
      });

      if (result?.canceled) return;

      const asset = result?.assets?.[0];
      if (!asset?.uri) return;

      const fileName = asset.name || `file_${Date.now()}`;
      await processAndUpload(asset.uri, fileName, asset.mimeType);
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Picker failed: " + (err?.message || "Unknown error"));
    } finally {
      unlockPicker();
    }
  };

  const handlePickImage = async (useCamera = false) => {
    if (!lockPicker()) return;

    try {
      closeMenu();

      const permission = useCamera
        ? await ImagePicker.requestCameraPermissionsAsync()
        : await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();

      if (!permission.granted) {
        Alert.alert("Permission Denied", "Please allow access in settings.");
        return;
      }

      await wait(200);

      const options = {
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 0.7,
        allowsEditing: false,
      };

      const result = useCamera
        ? await ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync(options)
        : await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync(options);

      if (result?.canceled) return;

      const asset = result?.assets?.[0];
      if (!asset?.uri) return;

      let fileName = asset.fileName;
      if (!fileName) {
        const uriParts = String(asset.uri).split("/");
        fileName = uriParts[uriParts.length - 1] || `photo_${Date.now()}.jpg`;
      }
      if (fileName && !fileName.includes(".")) fileName += ".jpg";

      await processAndUpload(asset.uri, fileName, asset.mimeType || "image/jpeg");
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Action failed: " + (err?.message || "Unknown error"));
    } finally {
      unlockPicker();
    }
  };

  const handleCreateSubmit = async () => {
    if (!name.trim()) return;

    if (!token) {
      Alert.alert("Not logged in", "No token found.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const isFolder = modalType === "createfolder";
      const trimmed = name.trim();
      const finalName = isFolder
        ? trimmed
        : trimmed.endsWith(".txt")
          ? trimmed
          : `${trimmed}.txt`;

      await createItem(token, {
        name: finalName,
        type: isFolder ? "folder" : "file",
        parentId: parentId,
        content: isFolder ? "" : String(content || "").replaceAll("\n", NEWLINE),
        mimeType: isFolder ? "" : "text/plain",
      });

      closeCreate();

      emitDriveRefresh({ reason: "create", name: finalName, type: isFolder ? "folder" : "file" });
      onRefresh?.();
    } catch (err) {
      Alert.alert("Error", err?.message || "Unknown error");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      unlockPicker();
    }
  };

  const disableActions = loading;

  return (
    <>
      {loading && (
        <View style={styles.loaderOverlay}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#0d6efd" />
          <Text style={styles.loaderText}>WORKING...</Text>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* OPEN MENU */}
      <Modal visible={isOpen} transparent animationType="none">
        <View style={styles.backdrop}>
          <BlurView intensity={22} tint="light" style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />
          <Pressable style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} onPress={() => { closeMenu(); unlockPicker(); }} />

          <View style={styles.menuContainer}>
            <MenuOption
              icon="folder-outline"
              label="New Folder"
              disabled={disableActions}
              onPress={() => {
                closeMenu();
                setModalType("createfolder");
              }}
            />
            <MenuOption
              icon="document-text-outline"
              label="New File"
              disabled={disableActions}
              onPress={() => {
                closeMenu();
                setModalType("createfile");
              }}
            />
            <MenuOption icon="image-outline" label="Upload Pic" disabled={disableActions} onPress={() => handlePickImage(false)} />
            <MenuOption icon="camera-outline" label="Take Photo" disabled={disableActions} onPress={() => handlePickImage(true)} />
            <MenuOption icon="cloud-upload-outline" label="Import File" disabled={disableActions} onPress={handlePickDocument} />
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* MAIN BUTTON */}
      {!isOpen && (
        <View style={styles.fabPosition}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.fabMain, disableActions && { opacity: 0.7 }]}
            onPress={() => {
              if (loading) return;
              if (pickerInProgressRef.current) return;
              setIsOpen(true);
            }}
            activeOpacity={0.9}
            disabled={disableActions}
          >
            <Ionicons name="add" size={35} color="white" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* CREATE MODAL */}
      <Modal visible={!!modalType} transparent animationType="slide">
        <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
          <Pressable style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} onPress={closeCreate} />
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>
              {modalType === "createfolder" ? "CREATE NEW FOLDER" : "CREATE NEW FILE"}
            </Text>

            <TextInput
              style={styles.input}
              placeholder={modalType === "createfolder" ? "Folder Name" : "File Name"}
              value={name}
              onChangeText={setName}
              autoFocus
              returnKeyType="done"
              onSubmitEditing={Keyboard.dismiss}
            />

            {/* Parent Folder Selector */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.folderSelector}
              onPress={() => setShowFolderPicker(true)}
            >
              <Ionicons name="folder-outline" size={20} color="#666" />
              <Text style={styles.folderSelectorText} numberOfLines={1}>
                {selectedFolderName}
              </Text>
              <Ionicons name="chevron-down" size={20} color="#666" />
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {/* Folder Picker Dropdown */}
            {showFolderPicker && (
              <View style={styles.folderDropdown}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[
                    styles.folderOption,
                    parentId === null && styles.folderOptionSelected,
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setParentId(null);
                    setShowFolderPicker(false);
                  }}
                >
                  <Ionicons name="home-outline" size={18} color="#0d6efd" />
                  <Text style={styles.folderOptionText}>Root (My Drive)</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <FlatList
                  data={folders}
                  keyExtractor={(item) => item.id || item._id}
                  style={{ maxHeight: 150 }}
                  renderItem={({ item: folder }) => (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={[
                        styles.folderOption,
                        (parentId === folder.id || parentId === folder._id) &&
                        styles.folderOptionSelected,
                      ]}
                      onPress={() => {
                        setParentId(folder.id || folder._id);
                        setShowFolderPicker(false);
                      }}
                    >
                      <Ionicons name="folder" size={18} color="#f4b400" />
                      <Text style={styles.folderOptionText} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {folder.name}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}
                  ListEmptyComponent={
                    <Text style={styles.noFolders}>No folders available</Text>
                  }
                />
              </View>
            )}

            {modalType === "createfile" && (
              <ScrollView style={{ width: "100%" }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
                <TextInput
                  style={[styles.input, { height: 120, textAlignVertical: "top" }]}
                  placeholder="Content..."
                  value={content}
                  onChangeText={setContent}
                  multiline
                />
              </ScrollView>
            )}

            <View style={styles.modalButtons}>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={closeCreate} disabled={loading}>
                <Text style={styles.cancelText}>CANCEL</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.createBtn, loading && { opacity: 0.7 }]}
                onPress={handleCreateSubmit}
                disabled={loading}
              >
                <Text style={styles.confirmText}>CREATE</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
}

function MenuOption({ icon, label, onPress, disabled }) {
  return (
    <TouchableOpacity
      style={[styles.singleContainer, disabled && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
      onPress={disabled ? undefined : onPress}
      activeOpacity={0.9}
    >
      <Ionicons name={icon} size={22} color="#444" style={{ marginRight: 12 }} />
      <Text style={styles.labelText} numberOfLines={1} ellipsizeMode="clip">
        {label.toUpperCase()}
      </Text>
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, TouchableOpacity, Alert } from 'react-native';
import { DrawerContentScrollView } from '@react-navigation/drawer';
import { useRouter, usePathname } from 'expo-router';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { BlurView } from 'expo-blur';
import { useAuth } from '../app/providers/AuthContext'; 
import { getUsage } from '../features/drive/services/fileService';
import StorageTree from '../features/drive/components/StorageTree';
import { useTheme } from '../app/providers/ThemeContext';

// ג… ׳ ׳×׳™׳‘ ׳׳×׳•׳§׳: ׳-src/layout ׳-src/shared_code
import MusicToggle from '../shared_code/components/MusicToggle';

const MENU_ITEMS = [
  { id: 'home', icon: 'home', label: 'Home', route: '/home' },
  { id: 'recent', icon: 'clock', label: 'Recent', route: '/recent' },
  { id: 'important', icon: 'alert-circle', label: 'Important', route: '/important' },
  { id: 'trash', icon: 'trash-2', label: 'Trash', route: '/trash' },
  { id: 'storage', icon: 'hard-drive', label: 'Storage', route: '/storage' },
];

export default function SideBar(props) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { token, logout } = useAuth();
  const { isDarkMode, toggleTheme, colors } = useTheme();
  
  const [usage, setUsage] = useState({ usedBytes: 0, quotaBytes: 15 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024 });

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    async function loadUsage() {
      if (!token) return;
      try {
        const u = await getUsage(token);
        if (!alive) return;
        if (u) setUsage({ usedBytes: u.usedBytes ?? 0, quotaBytes: u.quotaBytes ?? 15 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024 });
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    loadUsage();
    const interval = setInterval(loadUsage, 10000); 
    return () => { alive = false; clearInterval(interval); };
  }, [token]);

  const handleLogoutAction = () => {
    Alert.alert("Logout", "Are you sure?", [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      { text: "Logout", style: "destructive", onPress: async () => await logout() }
    ]);
  };

  return (
    <BlurView intensity={80} tint={isDarkMode ? "dark" : "light"} style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Text style={[styles.headerTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>CopaDrive</Text>
      </View>

      <DrawerContentScrollView {...props} contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
        <View style={styles.list}>
          {MENU_ITEMS.map((item) => {
            const isActive = pathname.includes(item.route);
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={item.id}
                onPress={() => router.push(item.route)}
                style={[styles.item, isActive && { backgroundColor: colors.activeItem }]}
              >
                <Feather name={item.icon} size={20} color={isActive ? colors.primary : colors.iconColor} />
                <Text style={[styles.text, { color: isActive ? colors.primary : colors.textPrimary }, isActive && styles.textActive]}>
                    {item.label}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}

          <View style={[styles.divider, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />

          <TouchableOpacity onPress={handleLogoutAction} style={styles.logoutItem}>
            <Feather name="log-out" size={20} color={colors.danger} />
            <Text style={[styles.logoutText, { color: colors.danger }]}>Logout</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </DrawerContentScrollView>
      
      <View style={[styles.footer, { borderTopColor: colors.border }]}>
          <View style={styles.controlsRow}>
             
             {/* ׳›׳₪׳×׳•׳¨ ׳”׳ ׳’׳ ׳”׳—׳“׳© */}
             <MusicToggle />

             <TouchableOpacity 
                style={[styles.themeBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderColor: colors.border }]}
                onPress={toggleTheme}
             >
                <Feather name={isDarkMode ? "sun" : "moon"} size={20} color={colors.textPrimary} />
             </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
          <StorageTree usedBytes={usage.usedBytes} quotaBytes={usage.quotaBytes} />
      </View>
    </BlurView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1 },
  header: { paddingTop: 60, paddingHorizontal: 24, paddingBottom: 20, marginBottom: 10 },
  headerTitle: { fontSize: 24, fontFamily: 'BrunoAceSC_400Regular', letterSpacing: 1.5 },
  scrollContent: { paddingTop: 0 },
  list: { paddingHorizontal: 16, gap: 6 },
  item: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', height: 48, paddingLeft: 16, borderRadius: 24, marginBottom: 4 },
  text: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: '500', marginLeft: 16 },
  textActive: { fontWeight: '700' },
  divider: { height: 1, marginVertical: 15, marginHorizontal: 10 },
  logoutItem: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', height: 48, paddingLeft: 16 },
  logoutText: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: '600', marginLeft: 16 },
  footer: { paddingBottom: 20, paddingTop: 10, borderTopWidth: 1 },
  controlsRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingHorizontal: 20, marginBottom: 15, alignItems: 'center' },
  themeBtn: { width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 20, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', borderWidth: 1 }
});

===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\shared_code\hooks\api\request.js =====
import Constants from "expo-constants";

// ׳₪׳•׳ ׳§׳¦׳™׳” ׳—׳›׳׳” ׳©׳׳—׳׳¦׳× ׳׳× ׳”-IP ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳—׳©׳‘ ׳©׳׳¨׳™׳¥ ׳׳× ׳”-Expo
const getBaseUrl = () => {
  // ׳׳ ׳”׳•׳’׳“׳¨ ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳” ׳¡׳‘׳™׳‘׳” ׳™׳“׳ ׳™ (׳׳׳©׳ ׳‘׳₪׳¨׳•׳“׳§׳©׳) - ׳ ׳©׳×׳׳© ׳‘׳•
  if (process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL) {
    return process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/api\/?$/, "").replace(/\/$/, "");
  }

  // ׳׳—׳¨׳×, ׳ ׳©׳×׳׳© ׳‘׳›׳×׳•׳‘׳× ׳©׳ ׳”-Debugger ׳©׳ Expo (׳–׳” ׳‘׳¢׳¦׳ ׳”-IP ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳—׳©׳‘ ׳©׳׳ ׳‘׳¨׳©׳× ׳”-Wifi)
  const debuggerHost = Constants.expoConfig?.hostUri;
  const localhost = debuggerHost?.split(":")[0];

  if (!localhost) {
    // Fallback ׳׳׳§׳¨׳” ׳§׳™׳¦׳•׳ (׳›׳׳• ׳¡׳™׳׳•׳׳˜׳•׳¨ ׳׳§׳•׳׳™)
    return "http://localhost:3000";
  }

  // ׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”׳›׳×׳•׳‘׳× ׳”׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™׳× ׳¢׳ ׳”׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜ ׳©׳ ׳”׳©׳¨׳× (3000)
  return `http://${localhost}:3000`;
};

const API_BASE_URL = getBaseUrl();

console.log("נ”§ Dynamic API_BASE_URL:", API_BASE_URL);

export async function request(path, options = {}) {
  // Ensure path starts with /
  const normalizedPath = path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`;
  
  // Build the full URL
  const url = `${API_BASE_URL}${normalizedPath}`;
  
  console.log("נ“¡ Request:", options.method || "GET", url);

  try {
    const isFormData = options.body instanceof FormData;

    const headers = {
      ...(isFormData ? {} : { "Content-Type": "application/json" }),
      ...(options.headers || {}),
    };

    if (options.token) {
      headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${options.token}`;
    }

    const res = await fetch(url, {
      ...options,
      headers,
      body: isFormData ? options.body : (options.body ? JSON.stringify(options.body) : undefined),
    });

    const text = await res.text();
    let data = null;
    try {
      data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
    } catch {
      data = text;
    }

    if (!res.ok) {
      const msg =
        (data && (data.message || data.error)) ||
        (typeof data === "string" && data) ||
        `Request failed (${res.status})`;
      throw new Error(msg);
    }

    return data;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Request Error:", error?.message || error);
    throw error;
  }
}

===== C:\Users\Yocha\Desktop\Studies BIU\SemesterC\AP\CopaDrive\mobile-client\src\shared_code\utils\formatBytes.js =====
// src/shared_code/utils/formatBytes.js

export function formatBytes(bytes, decimals = 1) {
  const n = Number(bytes);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return "0 B";

  const k = 1024;
  const sizes = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
  const i = Math.min(Math.floor(Math.log(n) / Math.log(k)), sizes.length - 1);

  const value = n / Math.pow(k, i);
  // ׳׳ ׳”׳׳¡׳₪׳¨ ׳©׳׳, ׳׳ ׳׳¦׳™׳’׳™׳ ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳” ׳¢׳©׳¨׳•׳ ׳™׳×
  const fixed = Number.isInteger(value) ? 0 : decimals;
  
  return `${value.toFixed(fixed)} ${sizes[i]}`;
}
